Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is the primary cause of cervical cancer and its precursors, yet remains a major global health burden despite vaccination and screening advances. This review was needed to synthesize current knowledge on HPV virology, pathogenesis, diagnostic biomarkers, and evolving management strategies.
Design and population
The study is a comprehensive literature review synthesizing current evidence on high-risk HPV types, particularly HPV16 and HPV18, and their associated precancerous lesions. It evaluates diagnostic and therapeutic approaches without specifying a defined population or study setting.
Core methods
The review synthesizes literature on HPV oncogene mechanisms (E6 and E7), multi-modal screening protocols, and therapeutic interventions including surgical excisions, thermal ablation, photodynamic therapy, and topical immunomodulators such as imiquimod. It also examines molecular markers (p16/Ki-67, DNA methylation signatures, circulating microRNAs) and microbiome-targeted adjunctive therapies.
Main results
The review reports that large loop excision of the transformation zone (LLETZ) remains the gold standard for high-grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN) but is associated with obstetric complications, necessitating fertility-sparing alternatives. Non-invasive modalities such as thermal ablation, photodynamic therapy, and imiquimod achieve high rates of lesion regression and viral clearance while preserving cervical integrity.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence that HPV-associated precancerous lesion management is shifting toward personalized, molecularly guided, and fertility-preserving strategies. They highlight the integration of advanced diagnostic biomarkers and minimally invasive therapies as novel developments in the field.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support expanding global vaccine access and implementing multi-modal, minimally invasive therapeutic strategies to reduce cervical cancer burden, particularly in low- and middle-income countries. The review underscores the importance of molecular diagnostics and fertility-sparing treatments in improving patient outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report study design limitations, bias, confounding, or sample size constraints specific to primary data. As an abstract-only review, the strength of evidence is limited by reliance on synthesized literature rather than original empirical data, warranting a moderate confidence assessment.

高危型HPV与宫颈细胞学相关性在古吉拉特邦女性中的患病率:对区域筛查和预防策略的启示

Prevalence of high-risk human papillomavirus and cytological correlation in Gujarati women: implications for regional screening and vaccination strategies
作者： Anand Shah, Bijal Patel, Majal Shah, Ruchi Arora, Chetna Parikh, Rohini Patel, Mohit Makwana, Samanta Satarupa, Vibha Vyas, Vaishali Ravani
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 尽管有预防策略,宫颈癌仍然是印度的一个公共卫生问题。高危型HPV(hrHPV)感染是宫颈癌的主要病因。人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)-16和HPV-18是导致癌症的最常见致癌亚型。本研究旨在估计古吉拉特邦女性中高危型HPV基因型的患病率,并评估其与宫颈细胞学异常之间的关联。方法 本研究在2022年1月至2023年12月期间,对参加古吉拉特癌症研究所在社区组织的筛查活动的9,706名≥30岁的已婚女性进行了横断面研究。使用Roche COBAS ® 4800平台采集宫颈样本以检测hrHPV DNA,并在hrHPV阳性病例中进行液基细胞学检查。统计分析用于评估特定基因型的患病率及其与人口统计学和临床变量的关联。结果 总体而言,4.9%(n=478)的女性为hrHPV阳性,其中非16/18型hrHPV占感染的大多数(62.8%),其次是HPV-16(18%)和HPV-18(8.4%)。多变量分析显示,早婚、早孕以及体检中发现的宫颈异常与hrHPV阳性独立相关,其中恶性宫颈发现与hrHPV阳性关联最强(调整后的比值比[OR]=38.2;95% CI: 3.4–429.1),而早孕则带来约四倍的风险(调整后的OR=4.29;95% CI: 2.31–7.96)。HPV-16与细胞学异常,特别是高级别鳞状上皮内病变(HSIL)关联最强(OR=6.18;95% CI: 3.10–12.4),而HPV-16/18共感染进一步增加了HSIL的风险(OR=31.33)。结论 非16/18型高危型HPV基因型的主导地位,加上HPV-16与HSIL的强关联,突显了在古吉拉特邦实施扩展基因型监测和基于基因型的筛查策略的重要性。此外,强调了生殖健康干预措施(如防止早婚和早孕)的必要性。这些发现直接支持世界卫生组织(WHO)的宫颈癌消除策略,并呼吁采取政策行动,扩大筛查覆盖范围,加强监测,并在古吉拉特邦实施针对性的预防策略。

问题与背景
尽管有预防策略,宫颈癌仍然是印度的一个公共卫生问题,而高危型HPV感染是其主要病因。本研究旨在填补关于高危型HPV基因型患病率及其与古吉拉特邦女性宫颈细胞学异常关联的知识空白。
设计与对象
本研究在2022年1月至2023年12月期间,对参加古吉拉特癌症研究所在社区组织的筛查活动的9,706名≥30岁的已婚女性进行了横断面研究。
核心方法
宫颈样本使用Roche COBAS 4800平台进行高危型HPV DNA检测,并在高危型HPV阳性病例中进行液基细胞学检查。统计分析用于评估特定基因型的患病率及其与人口统计学和临床变量的关联。
主要结果
总体而言,4.9%(n=478)的女性为高危型HPV阳性,其中非16/18型高危型HPV占感染的大多数(62.8%),其次是HPV-16(18%)和HPV-18(8.4%)。HPV-16与高级别鳞状上皮内病变(HSIL)关联最强(OR=6.18;95% CI: 3.10–12.4),而HPV-16/18共感染进一步增加了HSIL的风险(OR=31.33)。
解释与创新
非16/18型高危型HPV基因型的主导地位,加上HPV-16与HSIL的强关联,突显了在古吉拉特邦实施扩展基因型监测和基于基因型的筛查策略的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现通过支持扩大筛查覆盖范围、加强监测以及在古吉拉特邦实施针对性的预防策略,直接支持了世界卫生组织(WHO)的宫颈癌消除策略。
局限与证据强度
横断面研究设计限制了因果推论的可能,且本研究聚焦于≥30岁的已婚女性,可能降低其对年轻或未婚人群的推广性。由于社区筛查活动可能存在选择偏倚,因此证据强度为中等。

Prevalence of high-risk human papillomavirus and cytological correlation in Gujarati women: implications for regional screening and vaccination strategies

Authors: Anand Shah, Bijal Patel, Majal Shah, Ruchi Arora, Chetna Parikh, Rohini Patel, Mohit Makwana, Samanta Satarupa, Vibha Vyas, Vaishali Ravani
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background High-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) infection is a major etiological factor for cervical cancer. HPV-16 and HPV-18 are the most prevalent oncogenic subtypes of cancer. Despite the availability of preventive strategies, cervical cancer remains a public health concern in India. This study aimed to estimate the prevalence of hrHPV genotypes and evaluate their association with cervical cytological abnormalities among women in Gujarat, India. Methods A cross-sectional study was conducted among 9,706 married women aged ≥ 30 years who attended community-based screening camps organized by the Gujarat Cancer Research Institute between January 2022 and December 2023. Cervical samples were collected for hrHPV DNA detection using the Roche COBAS ® 4800 platform, and liquid-based cytology was performed in hrHPV-positive cases. Statistical analysis was used to assess genotype-specific prevalence and associations with demographic and clinical variables. Results Overall, 4.9% ( n = 478) of women were hrHPV positive, with non-16/18 hrHPV types accounting for most infections (62.8%), followed by HPV-16 (18%) and HPV-18 (8.4%). Multivariate analysis revealed that early age at marriage, early age at first pregnancy, and abnormal cervical findings on examination were independently associated with hrHPV positivity, with malignant cervical findings showing the strongest association (adjusted odds ratio [OR] = 38.2; 95% CI: 3.4–429.1) and early pregnancy conferring an approximately fourfold risk (adjusted OR = 4.29; 95% CI: 2.31–7.96). HPV-16 showed the strongest association with cytological abnormality, particularly high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (HSIL) (OR = 6.18; 95% CI: 3.10–12.4), and HPV-16/18 co-infection further increased the risk of HSIL (OR = 31.33). Conclusion The predominance of non-16/18 hrHPV genotypes, combined with the strong HPV-16–HSIL association, underscores the importance of extended-genotype vaccination, genotype-informed screening, and reproductive health interventions (e.g., preventing early marriage and pregnancy). These findings directly support the WHO’s cervical cancer elimination strategy and call for policy actions to expand vaccine coverage, strengthen screening, and implement context-specific prevention strategies in Gujarat.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer remains a public health concern in India despite available preventive strategies, and high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) infection is a major etiological factor. The study addressed the knowledge gap regarding the prevalence of hrHPV genotypes and their association with cervical cytological abnormalities among women in Gujarat.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 9,706 married women aged 30 years or older who attended community-based screening camps organized by the Gujarat Cancer Research Institute between January 2022 and December 2023.
Core methods
Cervical samples were collected for hrHPV DNA detection using the Roche COBAS 4800 platform, and liquid-based cytology was performed in hrHPV-positive cases. Statistical analysis assessed genotype-specific prevalence and associations with demographic and clinical variables.
Main results
Overall, 4.9% (n=478) of women were hrHPV positive, with non-16/18 hrHPV types accounting for 62.8% of infections, followed by HPV-16 (18%) and HPV-18 (8.4%). HPV-16 showed the strongest association with high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (HSIL) (OR=6.18; 95% CI: 3.10–12.4), and HPV-16/18 co-infection increased HSIL risk (OR=31.33).
Interpretation and novelty
The predominance of non-16/18 hrHPV genotypes combined with the strong association of HPV-16 with HSIL highlights the need for extended-genotype vaccination and genotype-informed screening strategies in Gujarat.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the WHO’s cervical cancer elimination strategy by informing policy actions to expand vaccine coverage, strengthen screening, and implement context-specific prevention strategies in Gujarat.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and the study’s focus on married women aged 30 years or older may reduce generalisability to younger or unmarried populations. The evidence strength is moderate due to potential selection bias in community-based screening camps.

免疫应答和疫苗接种后造血干细胞移植青少年人乳头瘤病毒抗体的持久性:一项试点研究

Immune response and durability of human papillomavirus antibodies in vaccinated adolescents after hematopoietic stem cell transplantation: a pilot study
作者： Benjaporn Wangviboonchai, Phuwakrit Nithirungruang, Usanarat Anurathapan, Samart Pakakasama, Suradej Hongeng, Chompunuch Klinmalai, Nopporn Apiwattanakul
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估HPV 疫苗接种疫苗在完成造血干细胞移植(HSCT)的青少年中的免疫原性,因为HSCT会清除疫苗免疫记忆细胞,因此这一人群需要重新接种疫苗。
设计与对象
本研究包括两种队列研究方案:队列研究 1是一项准实验研究,评估在HSCT后9至15岁青少年中接种2剂HPV 疫苗接种疫苗后的血清转换情况;而队列研究 2是一项横断面研究,评估在完成疫苗接种疫苗系列后不同时间间隔内接种疫苗的HSCT后青少年中抗体的持续性。共有23名参与者纳入队列研究 1,15名纳入队列研究 2。
核心方法
在队列研究 1中,于首次接种后6个月及第二次接种后1个月,检测了抗HPV16 L1 IgG及抗HPV18 L1 IgG水平。在队列研究 2中,对完成疫苗接种疫苗接种系列6至62个月前的参与者进行了抗体持久性评估。
主要结果
在接种第一剂HPV 疫苗疫苗后6个月,54.5%的参与者对HPV 16产生了血清转换,而68.2%的参与者对HPV 18产生了血清转换。在接种第二剂后1个月,这些比例分别上升至95.5%和90.9%。在队列研究 2中,所有参与者均检测到针对HPV16的抗体,除1人外,其余均检测到针对HPV18的抗体。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,在HSCT后青少年中,2剂HPV 疫苗接种疫苗接种方案可能诱导更好的抗体应答,这是在这一特定免疫功能低下人群中的一项新发现。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为HSCT后青少年中2剂HPV 疫苗接种疫苗接种方案的有效性提供了初步证据,这可能有助于制定未来的疫苗接种策略,并对这一高风险人群进行长期免疫监测。
局限与证据强度
本研究是一项试点研究,样本量较小,证据仅限于抗体水平,缺乏长期结果数据。由于研究设计和范围有限,这些发现的可信度中等。

Immune response and durability of human papillomavirus antibodies in vaccinated adolescents after hematopoietic stem cell transplantation: a pilot study

Authors: Benjaporn Wangviboonchai, Phuwakrit Nithirungruang, Usanarat Anurathapan, Samart Pakakasama, Suradej Hongeng, Chompunuch Klinmalai, Nopporn Apiwattanakul
Original abstract (English)
Hematopoietic stem cell transplantation (HSCT), preceded by chemotherapy and/or total body irradiation, eradicates hematopoietic cells, including vaccine-primed memory cells. Revaccination is therefore required after HSCT. Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination is particularly important in this population because of the increased risk of HPV-associated anogenital and oropharyngeal cancers. While the World Health Organization (WHO) currently recommends a single HPV vaccine dose for immunocompetent adolescents, 3 doses are recommended for immunocompromised individuals. To evaluate the immunogenicity of HPV vaccination in adolescents following HSCT. Two cohorts were studied. Cohort 1 was a quasi-experimental study assessing seroconversion after a 2-dose schedule of HPV vaccination in post-HSCT adolescents aged 9–15 years. Eligible participants were ≥ 1 year post-HSCT and had discontinued immunosuppressive therapy for ≥ 6 months. Participants received 2-dose quadrivalent HPV (qHPV) vaccine at 0 and 6 months. Anti-HPV16 L1 IgG and anti-HPV18 L1 IgG levels were measured 6 months after the first dose and 1 month after the second dose. Cohort 2 was a cross-sectional study evaluating the persistence of anti-HPV16 and anti-HPV18 antibodies in vaccinated post-HSCT adolescents at varying intervals after completion of the vaccination series. Twenty-three participants were enrolled in Cohort 1. Six months after the first vaccine dose, the proportions who seroconverted were 54.5% for HPV 16 and 68.2% for HPV 18. One month after the second dose, the proportions who seroconverted increased to 95.5% for HPV 16 and 90.9% for HPV 18. Local adverse events occurred in 22–39% of participants within 48 h after vaccination, whereas systemic adverse events were infrequent. No serious adverse events were observed. In Cohort 2, 15 participants were included. The interval since the last vaccine dose ranged from 6 to 62 months. All participants had detectable antibodies against HPV16, and all but one had detectable antibodies against HPV18. The findings from this pilot study provide preliminary evidence that a 2-dose HPV vaccination schedule may induce a better antibody response in adolescents after HSCT; however, further studies with larger sample sizes and serial assessment of antibody titers are needed to determine the optimal dosing strategy and long-term durability of immunity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the immunogenicity of HPV vaccination in adolescents who had undergone hematopoietic stem cell transplantation (HSCT), as HSCT eradicates vaccine-primed memory cells and revaccination is required in this population.
Design and population
The study included two cohorts: Cohort 1 was a quasi-experimental study assessing seroconversion after a 2-dose schedule of HPV vaccination in post-HSCT adolescents aged 9–15 years, while Cohort 2 was a cross-sectional study evaluating antibody persistence in vaccinated post-HSCT adolescents at varying intervals post-vaccination. Twenty-three participants were enrolled in Cohort 1, and 15 in Cohort 2.
Core methods
Anti-HPV16 L1 IgG and anti-HPV18 L1 IgG levels were measured 6 months after the first dose and 1 month after the second dose in Cohort 1. In Cohort 2, antibody persistence was evaluated in participants whose vaccination series had been completed between 6 and 62 months prior.
Main results
Six months after the first vaccine dose, 54.5% of participants seroconverted for HPV 16 and 68.2% for HPV 18. One month after the second dose, these proportions increased to 95.5% for HPV 16 and 90.9% for HPV 18. In Cohort 2, all participants had detectable antibodies against HPV16, and all but one had detectable antibodies against HPV18.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that a 2-dose HPV vaccination schedule may induce a better antibody response in adolescents after HSCT, which is a novel observation in this specific immunocompromised population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide preliminary evidence for the effectiveness of a 2-dose HPV vaccination schedule in post-HSCT adolescents, which could inform future vaccination strategies and long-term immunity monitoring in this high-risk group.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a pilot with a small sample size, and the evidence is limited to antibody levels without long-term outcome data. The confidence in these findings is moderate due to the study's design and limited scope.

治疗治疗HPV16/18感染持续性宫颈低度鳞状上皮内病变的对照研究

A comparative study of treatment of persistent cervical low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions with HPV16/18 infection.
作者： Lingjia Lu, Chenfeng Xu, Ruizhe Chen, Weiguo Lv, Yunfeng Fu
摘要中文翻译目的:目前针对 HPV16/18 感染的持续性宫颈低度鳞状上皮内病变 (LSIL) 的 治疗 方案有一定的局限性。本研究旨在评估 5-氨基乙酰丙酸光动力疗法 (ALA-PDT) 对这些患者的疗效。方法:对我院2023年1月至2024年12月门诊收治的304例持续性宫颈LSIL合并HPV16/18感染患者进行回顾性分析。根据收到的治疗,将患者分为三组:ALA-PDT组、环形电切手术(LEEP)组和观察组。在诊断和相应的治疗之后,患者被随访一年。比较三组之间的LSIL消退率、HPV16/18清除率和不良反应。结果:ALA-PDT、LEEP 和观察组分别入组了 90 例、42 例和 172 例患者。ALA-PDT、LEEP和观察组的宫颈LSIL消退率分别为85.56%、90.48%和56.98%。ALA-PDT与LEEP组之间差异无统计学意义,但均显着优于观察组。ALA-PDT、LEEP和观察组的HPV清除率分别为84.44%、78.57%和44.19%。同样,ALA-PDT和LEEP组之间差异无统计学意义,但均显着高于观察组。ALA-PDT 组的不良反应较轻微。LEEP组术后颈椎狭窄或粘连的发病率为21.43%。结论:对于 HPV16/18 感染的持续性宫颈 LSIL 患者,ALA-PDT 似乎是一种有效且安全的 治疗 选择,对于希望保留生育能力的年轻女性尤其重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
目前针对 HPV16/18 感染的持续性宫颈低度鳞状上皮内病变 (LSIL) 的 治疗 方案有一定的局限性。本研究旨在评估 5-氨基乙酰丙酸光动力疗法 (ALA-PDT) 对这些患者的疗效。
设计与对象
对2023年1月至2024年12月我院门诊收治的304例HPV16/18感染持续性宫颈LSIL患者进行回顾性分析。根据收到的治疗,将患者分为三组:ALA-PDT组、环形电切术(LEEP)组和观察组。在诊断和相应的治疗之后,患者被随访一年。
核心方法
对三组患者的低级别鳞状上皮内病变(LSIL)缓解率、人乳头瘤病毒16/18型(HPV16/18)清除率及不良反应进行了比较。该研究采用回顾性分析设计,并在治疗组间比较结局。
主要结果
ALA-PDT组、LEEP组及观察组分别纳入90例、42例及172例患者。ALA-PDT组、LEEP组及观察组的宫颈LSIL缓解率分别为85.56%、90.48%及56.98%。ALA-PDT组、LEEP组及观察组的人乳头瘤病毒清除率分别为84.44%、78.57%及44.19%。ALA-PDT组的不良反应程度较轻。LEEP组术后宫颈狭窄或粘连的发病率为21.43%。
解释与创新
ALA-PDT组与LEEP组间差异无统计学意义,但两组均显著优于观察组。研究得出结论:对于持续性宫颈LSIL伴HPV16/18感染的患者,ALA-PDT似乎是一种有效且安全的治疗选择。
科研与公卫意义
ALA-PDT可能为持续性宫颈LSIL伴HPV16/18感染的患者提供一种有效且安全的治疗选择,尤其适用于希望保留生育能力的年轻女性。
局限与证据强度
该研究为回顾性分析,可能引入偏倚并限制证据强度。由于研究性质为观察性研究,证据强度为中等。

A comparative study of treatment of persistent cervical low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions with HPV16/18 infection.

Authors: Lingjia Lu, Chenfeng Xu, Ruizhe Chen, Weiguo Lv, Yunfeng Fu
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: Current treatment options for persistent cervical low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (LSIL) with HPV16/18 infection have certain limitations. This study aims to evaluate the efficacy of 5-aminolevulinic acid photodynamic therapy (ALA-PDT) in these patients. METHODS: A retrospective analysis was conducted on 304 outpatients with persistent cervical LSIL with HPV16/18 infection in our hospital from January 2023 to December 2024. Based on the treatment received, patients were divided into three groups: ALA-PDT group, loop electrosurgical excision procedure (LEEP) group, and observation group. After diagnosis and corresponding treatment, patients were followed up for one year. The LSIL regression rates, HPV16/18 clearance rates, and adverse effects were compared among the three groups. RESULTS: The ALA-PDT, LEEP, and observation groups respectively enrolled 90, 42, and 172 patients. The cervical LSIL regression rates in the ALA-PDT, LEEP, and observation groups were 85.56%, 90.48%, and 56.98%, respectively. The difference between the ALA-PDT and LEEP groups was not statistically significant, but both were significantly superior to the observation group. The HPV clearance rates in the ALA-PDT, LEEP, and observation groups were 84.44%, 78.57%, and 44.19%, respectively. Similarly, the difference between the ALA-PDT and LEEP groups was not statistically significant, but both were significantly higher than the observation group. Adverse effects in the ALA-PDT group were mild. The incidence of postoperative cervical stenosis or adhesion in the LEEP group was 21.43%. CONCLUSION: ALA-PDT appears to be an effective and safe treatment option for patients with persistent cervical LSIL with HPV16/18 infection, and is particularly significant for young women who wish to preserve fertility.

Question and background
Current treatment options for persistent cervical low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (LSIL) with HPV16/18 infection have certain limitations. This study aims to evaluate the efficacy of 5-aminolevulinic acid photodynamic therapy (ALA-PDT) in these patients.
Design and population

After diagnosis and corresponding treatment, patients were followed up for one year.
Core methods
The LSIL regression rates, HPV16/18 clearance rates, and adverse effects were compared among the three groups. The study used a retrospective analysis design and compared outcomes across treatment groups.
Main results
The ALA-PDT, LEEP, and observation groups respectively enrolled 90, 42, and 172 patients. The cervical LSIL regression rates in the ALA-PDT, LEEP, and observation groups were 85.56%, 90.48%, and 56.98%, respectively. ALA-PDT may offer an effective and safe treatment option for patients with persistent cervical LSIL with HPV16/18 infection, particularly for young women who wish to preserve fertility.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a retrospective analysis, which may introduce bias and limit the strength of the evidence. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

印度迈索尔一家医院开展的高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)基因型分布研究:涉及细胞学正常女性及宫颈癌前病变的横断面调查

Distribution of high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) genotypes among women with normal cytology and cervical cancer: a hospital-based cross-sectional study in Mysore, India
作者： Medha Karnik, Venugopal R. Bovilla, G. Lakshmikantha, Vinayak D. Dendukuri, Mohammed Kaleem Ullah, Suma M. Nataraj, Habeeb Shaik Mohideen, C. Chaitra, Mukesh Shanthilal, B. Vijaya
摘要中文翻译宫颈癌 仍然是印度主要的公共卫生负担,高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 的持续感染被认为是主要病因。了解 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 基因型的分布对于优化筛查和 疫苗接种 策略至关重要。因此,本研究旨在确定印度卡纳塔克邦迈索尔三级护理医院的 20-75 岁女性的 患病率 和 HR-HPV 基因型分布。在这项以医院为基础的横断面研究中,收集了 2021 年 8 月至 2023 年 3 月期间在 JSS 医院和 Cheluvamba 医院妇产科或迈索尔 Krishnarajendra 医院肿瘤科就诊的 172 名 20-75 岁非孕妇的宫颈阴道灌洗 (CVL) 样本。Cheluvamba 医院和 Krishnarajendra 医院隶属于印度卡纳塔克邦卡纳塔克邦迈索尔医学院和研究所 (MMCRI)。使用巴氏(Pap)试验进行细胞学评估。通过 MY09/MY11 聚合酶链式反应 (PCR) 检测 HPV DNA,并使用 TRUPCR ® HR-HPV 基因分型试剂盒对 HPV 阳性参与者进行基因分型,该试剂盒可检测 14 种 高危型HPV 基因型。在 172 名参与者中,117 名(68.0%)细胞学正常,55 名(32.0%)出现细胞学异常或组织学证实为 宫颈癌。鳞状细胞癌(SCC)是最常见的异常,占异常病例的70.9%。47.0% 细胞学正常的女性和 80.0% 细胞学异常的女性中检测到 HPV DNA (p < 0.05)。基因分型分析显示,在细胞学正常的女性和 宫颈癌 女性中,HPV16 是主要的高危基因型。多种 HPV 基因型感染很常见,在细胞学正常的 HPV 阳性参与者中发生率为 49.1%,在 宫颈癌 病例中发生率为 59.5%。除 HPV16 外,HPV18、HPV31、HPV58 和 HPV59 也是最常检测到的高危基因型。这项基于医院的横断面研究深入了解了印度迈索尔细胞学正常的女性和 宫颈癌 女性中 高危型HPV 基因型的分布。HPV16 是两组的主要基因型,在大多数研究参与者中观察到多种 HPV 感染。这些发现有助于了解区域 HPV 基因型分布,并建议优化基于 HPV 的筛查策略和 疫苗接种 计划以获得更好的结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌在印度仍是一项主要的公共卫生负担,持续感染高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)被公认为其主要病因。了解高危型HPV基因型的分布对于优化筛查与抗病毒药物治疗策略至关重要。
设计与对象
本院基于社区的横断面研究收集了印度卡纳塔克邦迈索尔三级医院就诊的20–75岁172名非孕女性的宫颈阴道灌洗(CVL)样本。在172名参与者中,117人(68.0%)细胞学检查正常,而55人(32.0%)出现细胞学异常或组织学确诊的宫颈癌。
核心方法
通过 MY09/MY11 聚合酶链式反应 (PCR) 检测 HPV DNA,并使用 TRUPCR ® HR-HPV 基因分型试剂盒对 HPV 阳性参与者进行基因分型,该试剂盒可检测 14 种 高危型HPV 基因型。使用巴氏(Pap)试验进行细胞学评估。
主要结果
HPV DNA在细胞学正常女性中检出率为47.0%,在细胞学异常女性中为80.0%(p < 0.05)。基因分型分析显示,HPV16是高危型基因型在细胞学正常女性及宫颈癌前病变女性中的优势型别。
解释与创新
本研究结果有助于了解当地HPV基因型分布,并建议优化基于HPV的筛查策略与抗病毒药物治疗方案,以提升临床结局。
科研与公卫意义
本研究揭示了印度迈索尔地区细胞学正常女性及宫颈癌前病变人群中高危型HPV基因型的分布特征,可为靶向HPV筛查与抗病毒药物治疗方案的制定提供依据。
局限与证据强度
本研究局限于印度迈索尔单一医院环境,可能影响结果的外推性。由于横断面设计及潜在选择性偏倚,证据强度为中等。

Distribution of high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) genotypes among women with normal cytology and cervical cancer: a hospital-based cross-sectional study in Mysore, India

Authors: Medha Karnik, Venugopal R. Bovilla, G. Lakshmikantha, Vinayak D. Dendukuri, Mohammed Kaleem Ullah, Suma M. Nataraj, Habeeb Shaik Mohideen, C. Chaitra, Mukesh Shanthilal, B. Vijaya
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer remains a major public health burden in India, with persistent infection by high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) recognized as the primary etiological factor. Understanding the distribution of high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) genotypes is essential for optimizing screening and vaccination strategies. Therefore, this study aimed to determine the prevalence and genotype distribution of HR-HPV among women aged 20–75 years attending tertiary care hospitals in Mysore, Karnataka, India. In this hospital-based cross-sectional study, cervicovaginal lavage (CVL) samples were collected from 172 non-pregnant women aged 20–75 years attending the Obstetrics and Gynecology Departments of JSS Hospital and Cheluvamba Hospital, or the Oncology Department of Krishnarajendra Hospital, Mysore, between August 2021 and March 2023. Cheluvamba hospital and Krishnarajendra hospital belongs to Mysore Medical College and Research Institute (MMCRI), Govt of Karnataka, Karnataka, India. Cytological evaluation was performed using the Papanicolaou (Pap) test. HPV DNA was detected by MY09/MY11 polymerase chain reaction (PCR), and HPV-positive participants were genotyped using the TRUPCR ® HR-HPV Genotyping Kit, which detects 14 high-risk HPV genotypes. Of the 172 participants, 117 (68.0%) had normal cytology, while 55 (32.0%) presented with cytological abnormalities or histologically confirmed cervical cancer. Squamous cell carcinoma (SCC) was the most common abnormality, accounting for 70.9% of abnormal cases. HPV DNA was detected in 47.0% of women with normal cytology and 80.0% of women with abnormal cytology ( p < 0.05). Genotyping analysis revealed HPV16 as the predominant high-risk genotype in both women with normal cytology and those with cervical cancer. Multiple HPV genotype infections were common, occurring in 49.1% of HPV-positive participants with normal cytology and 59.5% of cervical cancer cases. In addition to HPV16, HPV18, HPV31, HPV58, and HPV59 were among the most frequently detected high-risk genotypes. This hospital-based cross-sectional study provides insights into the distribution of high-risk HPV genotypes among women with normal cytology and cervical cancer in Mysore, India. HPV16 was the predominant genotype in both groups, with multiple HPV infections observed in majority of study participants. These findings contribute to the understanding of regional HPV genotype distribution and recommend the optimization of HPV-based screening strategies and vaccination programs for better outcomes.

Question and background
Cervical cancer remains a major public health burden in India, with persistent infection by high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) recognized as the primary etiological factor. The study is limited to a single hospital-based setting in Mysore, India, which may affect generalisability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the cross-sectional design and potential for selection bias.

扩大青少年免疫覆盖:多年龄组人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗接种运动在西非——来自科特迪瓦、加纳、利比里亚和塞拉利昂的证据

Scaling Adolescent Immunity: Multi-Age Cohort Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Vaccination Campaigns in West Africa—Evidence from Côte d’Ivoire, Ghana, Liberia, and Sierra Leone
作者： Ado Mpia Bwaka, Sambo Guemgo, Marcellin Mengouo Nimpa, Pamela Mitula, Hermann Didi Ngossaki, Sylvain Honore Woromogo, Hadiatou Diallo, Milse William Nzingou Mouhembe, Crépin Hilaire Dadjo, Annick R. Ayele Dosseh
摘要中文翻译背景:宫颈癌 仍然是一个重大的公共卫生问题,每年造成约 660,000 例新病例和 348,000 例死亡,其中 90% 以上发生在低收入和中等收入国家。世卫组织非洲区域约占全球病例的 20% 和死亡人数的 30%。2020 年 11 月,世界卫生大会通过了 宫颈癌 消除全球战略,其中包括 2030 年 90-70-90 个目标。多年龄段 队列研究 (MAC) 运动已成为实现人口水平 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖的高影响力战略。本研究记录了 2025 年科特迪瓦、加纳、利比里亚和塞拉利昂 HPV MAC 运动的实施情况和成果。方法:这项多国混合方法分析将定量管理运动数据与世卫组织免疫准备情况评估工具中八个业务准备领域的定性规划评估相结合。数据是在 2025 年 1 月至 12 月期间从国家报告、世卫组织监督任务和合作伙伴报告中收集的。覆盖率分析使用通过独立监测进行三角测量的行政数据。公平分析使用学校入学数据和地理可达性指数。结果:活动针对 7,376,096 名 9-18 岁女孩,覆盖人数约为 6,306,294 人,总体覆盖率达 85.5%。科特迪瓦、加纳、利比里亚和塞拉利昂的行政覆盖率分别为 88.0%、84.5%、60.0% 和 100%。所有四个国家在所有评估领域均达到了 80% 的最低战备状态阈值。累计培训卫生技术人员35860人次,培训后能力得分由58.8%提高到89.5%。所有活动中的冷链功能均超过 98%。总共报告了 314 例免疫接种后不良事件 (AEFI),全部分类为轻度,每 100,000 剂疫苗中大约有 5.0 例。在校女童和失学女童之间的覆盖率差距为 18 至 35 个百分点。结论:研究结果表明,在政治承诺、多部门协调和充足的运营资源的支持下,大规模青少年 HPV 免疫接种可以在资源有限的环境中成功实施。Gavi 6.0 下的单剂量时间表过渡提供了简化给药的机会。优先行动包括将 HPV 纳入常规免疫接种、扩大失学女童的公平战略以​​及确保可持续融资。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 仍是一项重大公共卫生问题,每年导致约 660,000 例新发病例和 348,000 例死亡,其中 90% 以上发生在中低收入国家。本研究记录了 2025 年在科特迪瓦、加纳、利比里亚和塞拉利昂开展的 HPV 多年龄层 队列研究 疫苗接种运动的实施情况及结果。
设计与对象
多年龄段 队列研究 (MAC) 活动已成为实现人群水平 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖的高影响力策略。该活动针对 7,376,096 名 9-18 岁女孩,覆盖人数约 6,306,294 人,总体覆盖率达到 85.5%。
核心方法
数据收集时间为2025年1月至12月,来源包括国家报告、世界卫生组织督导任务及合作伙伴报告。覆盖率分析采用行政数据与独立监测的三角验证。公平性分析使用学校入学数据和地理可及性指数。
主要结果
科特迪瓦、加纳、利比里亚和塞拉利昂报告的行政覆盖率分别为88.0%、84.5%、60.0%和100%。四国在所有评估领域均达到最低80%运行就绪阈值。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,在政治承诺、多部门协调和充足的运营资源的支持下,大规模青少年 HPV 免疫接种可以在资源有限的环境中成功实施。Gavi 6.0 下的单剂量时间表过渡提供了简化给药的机会。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为在资源有限的环境中实施大规模青少年 HPV 免疫的可行性提供了证据,这可以为未来的 疫苗接种 策略和公共卫生干预措施提供信息。优先行动包括将 HPV 纳入常规免疫接种、扩大失学女童的公平战略以​​及确保可持续融资。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体限制。该研究提供了多个国家的详细覆盖范围和运营准备数据,因此证据强度很高。

Scaling Adolescent Immunity: Multi-Age Cohort Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Vaccination Campaigns in West Africa—Evidence from Côte d’Ivoire, Ghana, Liberia, and Sierra Leone

Authors: Ado Mpia Bwaka, Sambo Guemgo, Marcellin Mengouo Nimpa, Pamela Mitula, Hermann Didi Ngossaki, Sylvain Honore Woromogo, Hadiatou Diallo, Milse William Nzingou Mouhembe, Crépin Hilaire Dadjo, Annick R. Ayele Dosseh
Original abstract (English)
Background: Cervical cancer remains a major public health problem, causing approximately 660,000 new cases and 348,000 deaths annually, with more than 90% occurring in low- and middle-income countries. The WHO African Region accounts for approximately 20% of global cases and 30% of deaths. In November 2020, the World Health Assembly adopted the Global Strategy for Cervical Cancer Elimination with 90–70–90 targets for 2030. Multi-age cohort (MAC) campaigns have emerged as a high-impact strategy for achieving population-level HPV vaccination coverage. This study documents the implementation and outcomes of HPV MAC campaigns across Côte d’Ivoire, Ghana, Liberia, and Sierra Leone in 2025. Methods: This multi-country mixed-methods analysis integrated quantitative administrative campaign data with qualitative programmatic assessments across eight operational readiness domains from the WHO Immunization Readiness Assessment Tool. Data were collected between January and December 2025 from national reports, WHO supervision missions, and partner reports. Coverage analysis used administrative data triangulated with independent monitoring. Equity analysis used school enrolment data and geographic accessibility indices. Results: The campaigns targeted 7,376,096 girls aged 9–18 years and reached approximately 6,306,294, achieving 85.5% overall coverage. Côte d’Ivoire, Ghana, Liberia and Sierra Leone reported administrative coverage of 88.0%, 84.5%, 60.0% and 100%, respectively. All four countries achieved the minimum operational readiness threshold of 80% across all assessed domains. A total of 35,860 health workers were trained, with post-training competency scores increasing from 58.8% to 89.5%. Cold chain functionality exceeded 98% across all campaigns. A total of 314 cases of Adverse Events Following Immunization (AEFIs) were reported, all classified as mild, giving an approximate rate of 5.0 per 100,000 doses. Coverage gaps between in-school and out-of-school girls ranged from 18 to 35 percentage points. Conclusions: The findings suggest that large-scale adolescent HPV immunization can be successfully implemented in resource-limited settings when supported by political commitment, multisectoral coordination and adequate operational resources. The single-dose schedule transition under Gavi 6.0 offers opportunities to simplify delivery. Priority actions include integrating HPV into routine immunization, scaling equity strategies for out-of-school girls and ensuring sustainable financing.

The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations to the study design. The evidence strength is high as the study provides detailed coverage and operational readiness data across multiple countries.

父母对人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗的认知、态度及可接受度:对印度宫颈癌预防的启示

Parental knowledge, attitude and acceptability of Human Papillomavirus vaccine: implications for the prevention of cervical cancer in India
作者： Akanksha Singh, Varun Kumar S G, Anusree Prabhakaran, Prabhu Dutta Shaw, Arathi P. Rao
摘要中文翻译摘要 人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗接种是预防宫颈癌的关键手段;然而,尽管印度近期已通过公共卫生系统免费提供单剂次疫苗,HPV疫苗接种率仍处于低位。父母对HPV疫苗接种的接受度是确保适龄女孩疫苗接种的关键。因此,本研究在HPV疫苗接种早期推广阶段,对适龄女孩父母的HPV疫苗接种认知、态度及可接受度进行了评估。本社区横断面研究在印度HPV疫苗接种早期推广阶段对422名适龄女孩父母进行调查,评估其HPV疫苗接种认知、态度及可接受度。仅18.2%的参与者听说过HPV感染,6.6%识别HPV为宫颈癌的主要病因,8.5%听说过HPV疫苗,仅5.9%对疫苗接种表现出良好认知。尽管认知有限,48.8%持积极态度,62.6%表现出良好可接受度,72.7%表示愿意为子女接种疫苗。双变量分析显示,父母受教育程度较高及从事医疗相关职业与疫苗接种可接受度呈正相关。HPV感染认知与宫颈癌、HPV疫苗认知、态度及疫苗接种可接受度间存在显著正相关(p < 0.001)。研究结果凸显了认知缺口的持续存在,尽管可接受度处于中高水平。亟需通过社区卫生工作者(ASHAs)及数字平台开展有针对性的干预措施,以提升知情决策能力并支持弱势群体HPV疫苗接种的公平性,助力印度实现宫颈癌消除目标。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过探讨父母对适龄女孩HPV疫苗接种的认知、态度及可接受度,解决印度HPV疫苗接种率低的问题。由于HPV是宫颈癌的主要病因,此举对预防宫颈癌至关重要。
设计与对象
在印度 HPV 疫苗 推广的早期阶段,对 422 名青春期女孩的父母进行了一项基于社区的横断面研究,以评估他们对 HPV 疫苗接种 的知识、态度和可接受性。
核心方法
研究采用结构化问卷评估参与者对HPV感染、宫颈癌及HPV疫苗的认知。通过双变量分析探讨父母受教育程度、职业与HPV疫苗接种可接受度的关联。
主要结果
只有 18.2% 的参与者听说过 HPV 感染,6.6% 的参与者认为 HPV 是 宫颈癌 的主要原因,8.5% 的参与者听说过 HPV 疫苗。尽管了解有限,但48.8%的人持积极态度,62.6%的人表现出良好的接受度,72.7%的人表示愿意使用疫苗接种。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,尽管家长对 疫苗 的接受度为中等到高,但有关 HPV 及其与 宫颈癌 的关联的知识仍存在显着差距。这凸显了采取有针对性的干预措施以改善知情决策的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究深入了解了影响父母对 HPV 疫苗接种 可接受性的因素,这可以为提高 疫苗 吸收率和支持印度 宫颈癌 消除目标的策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系。证据是中等的,因为它基于自我报告的数据和双变量分析,没有控制混杂变量。

Parental knowledge, attitude and acceptability of Human Papillomavirus vaccine: implications for the prevention of cervical cancer in India

Authors: Akanksha Singh, Varun Kumar S G, Anusree Prabhakaran, Prabhu Dutta Shaw, Arathi P. Rao
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The Human Papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine is a key tool for cervical cancer prevention; however, vaccine uptake in India remains low despite its recent introduction as a free single-dose vaccine through the public health system. Parental acceptance to vaccination is key to ensure vaccine uptake of adolescent girls. Hence, this study was conducted to assess the knowledge, attitude and acceptability of HPV vaccination parents of adolescent girls, during the early phase of HPV vaccine rollout. This community-based cross-sectional study was conducted among 422 parents of adolescent girls, during the early phase of HPV vaccine rollout in the country, to assess their knowledge, attitude and acceptability of HPV vaccination. Only 18.2% of participants had heard of HPV infection, 6.6% identified HPV as the primary cause of cervical cancer, and 8.5% had heard of the HPV vaccine, with only 5.9% demonstrated good knowledge of vaccination. Despite limited knowledge, 48.8% had a positive attitude, 62.6% demonstrated good acceptability, and 72.7% expressed willingness for vaccination. Higher parental education and healthcare-related occupations were associated with greater vaccine acceptability in bivariate analysis. Significant positive correlations were observed among knowledge of HPV infection and cervical cancer, HPV vaccine knowledge, attitudes, and vaccine acceptability ( p < 0.001). These findings highlight persistent knowledge gaps despite moderate-to-high acceptability. Targeted interventions using community health workers (ASHAs) and digital platforms are needed to improve informed decision-making and support equitable HPV vaccine uptake in disadvantaged settings, contributing to India’s cervical cancer elimination goals.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the low HPV vaccine uptake in India by examining parental knowledge, attitude, and acceptability of HPV vaccination for adolescent girls. This is critical for preventing cervical cancer, as HPV is a primary cause of the disease.
Design and population
A community-based cross-sectional study was conducted among 422 parents of adolescent girls during the early phase of HPV vaccine rollout in India to assess their knowledge, attitude, and acceptability of HPV vaccination.
Core methods
The study used a structured questionnaire to evaluate participants' knowledge of HPV infection, cervical cancer, and the vaccine. Bivariate analysis was employed to examine associations between parental education, occupation, and vaccine acceptability.
Main results
Only 18.2% of participants had heard of HPV infection, 6.6% identified HPV as the primary cause of cervical cancer, and 8.5% had heard of the HPV vaccine. Despite limited knowledge, 48.8% had a positive attitude, 62.6% demonstrated good acceptability, and 72.7% expressed willingness for vaccination.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate a significant knowledge gap about HPV and its association with cervical cancer, even though there is moderate-to-high acceptability of the vaccine among parents. This highlights the need for targeted interventions to improve informed decision-making.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides insights into the factors influencing parental acceptability of HPV vaccination, which can inform strategies to enhance vaccine uptake and support India’s cervical cancer elimination goals.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on self-reported data and bivariate analysis without controlling for confounding variables.

智利中北部地区公共卫生系统中的患病率 人乳头瘤病毒

Prevalence of human papillomavirus in the public health system of the central-northern region of Chile
作者： Haylyn Sarria, Giuliano Bernal
摘要中文翻译目标 此前,我们发现在私人医疗系统就诊的女性中 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 的 患病率 较高。因此,本研究的目的是确定在公共卫生系统就诊的大量 队列研究 女性中 HR-HPV 的频率,并比较这些结果。方法 使用 qPCR 和巴氏技术对 2020 年至 2024 年间参加妇科检查的 18 至 91 岁女性的 39,512 份宫颈样本进行分析。结果 2020-2024年HR-HPV感染总体患病率为13.70%(95% CI:13.37-14.05%),2020年、2021年分别为13.07%、12.64%、14.80%、14.24%和14.15%。分别为 2022 年、2023 年和 2024 年。在25岁以下女性中,评估期间HR-HPV的患病率为34.03%,而25至44岁女性为16.35%,45至64岁女性为10.47%,64岁以上女性为11.52%。子宫颈抹片检查显示,HPV 阴性女性的 NILM 结果为 89.71%,而 HPV 阳性女性的 NILM 结果为 74.59%。HPV阴性女性中LSIL和HSIL的发生率分别为0.14%和0.07%;然而,在 HPV 阳性女性中,这些值分别增加到 4.73% 和 4.43%。HPV 阴性女性中只有 0.006% 可能患有癌症,而 HPV 阳性女性中则有 0.46% 可能患有癌症。结论 中北部地区公共卫生系统女性 HR-HPV 感染 患病率 似乎与智利之前报告的相似,但低于同一地区私营医疗系统女性报告的 患病率。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
需要进行这项研究来确定就读公共卫生系统的女性中 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 的频率,因为之前的研究发现私人卫生系统中的 患病率 较高。
设计与对象
一项横断面研究分析了智利中北部地区 2020 年至 2024 年间参加妇科检查的 18 至 91 岁女性的 39,512 份宫颈样本。
核心方法
使用 qPCR 和 Papanicolaou 技术对样本进行分析,以检测 HR-HPV 感染和细胞学异常。
主要结果
HR-HPV 感染的总体 患病率 为 13.70%(95% CI:13.37-14.05%),与年龄较大的群体相比,25 岁​​以下女性的感染率更高(34.03%)。HPV 阳性女性的 LSIL(4.73% vs. 0.14%)和 HSIL(4.43% vs. 0.07%)发生率显着高于 HPV 阴性女性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,公共卫生系统中的 HR-HPV 患病率 与之前报告的全国比率相似,但低于同一地区的私人卫生系统,突显了医疗系统之间 HPV 感染率的潜在差异。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果通过提供按年龄分层的 监测 数据为公共卫生 监测 和资源分配提供信息,这可以指导公共卫生系统中 HPV 相关疾病的有针对性的筛查和预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的优势在于其大样本量和纵向设计,但其局限性包括潜在的选择偏差,因为仅包括参加妇科检查的女性,并且由于报告仅具有摘要性质,证据并不充分。

Prevalence of human papillomavirus in the public health system of the central-northern region of Chile

Authors: Haylyn Sarria, Giuliano Bernal
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE Previously, we found a high prevalence of high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) in women attending the private health system. Therefore, the objective of this study was to determine the frequency of HR-HPV in a large cohort of women attending the public health system and to compare these findings. METHODS A total of 39,512 cervical samples from women aged 18 to 91 years who attended gynecological check-ups between 2020 and 2024 were analyzed using qPCR and the Papanicolaou technique. RESULTS The overall prevalence of HR-HPV infection between 2020 and 2024 was 13.70% (95% CI: 13.37-14.05%), with annual values of 13.07%, 12.64%, 14.80%, 14.24%, and 14.15% in 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, and 2024, respectively. In women under 25 years of age, the prevalence of HR-HPV was 34.03% during the evaluated period, compared to 16.35% in women aged 25 to 44 years, 10.47% in those aged 45 to 64 years, and 11.52% in women over 64 years of age. Pap smears showed 89.71% NILM results in HPV-negative women, compared to 74.59% in HPV-positive women. The rates of LSIL and HSIL were 0.14% and 0.07% in HPV-negative women, respectively; however, these values increased to 4.73% and 4.43% in HPV-positive women, respectively. Probable carcinoma was found in only 0.006% of HPV-negative women, but in 0.46% of HPV-positive women. CONCLUSIONS The prevalence of HR-HPV infection in women attending the public health system in the central-northern region appears to be similar to that previously reported in Chile, but lower than that reported in the same region for women attending the private health system.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to determine the frequency of high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) in women attending the public health system, as previous research had found a high prevalence in the private health system.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study analyzed 39,512 cervical samples from women aged 18 to 91 years who attended gynecological check-ups between 2020 and 2024 in the central-northern region of Chile.
Core methods
Samples were analyzed using qPCR and the Papanicolaou technique to detect HR-HPV infection and cytological abnormalities.
Main results
The overall prevalence of HR-HPV infection was 13.70% (95% CI: 13.37-14.05%), with higher rates in women under 25 years (34.03%) compared to older age groups. HPV-positive women had significantly higher rates of LSIL (4.73% vs. 0.14%) and HSIL (4.43% vs. 0.07%) than HPV-negative women.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that HR-HPV prevalence in the public health system was similar to previously reported national rates but lower than in the private health system in the same region, highlighting potential disparities in HPV infection rates between healthcare systems.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings inform public health surveillance and resource allocation by providing age-stratified prevalence data, which can guide targeted screening and prevention strategies for HPV-related diseases in the public health system.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's strength lies in its large sample size and longitudinal design, but limitations include potential selection bias as only women attending gynecological check-ups were included, and the evidence is moderate due to the abstract-only nature of the report.

03 | 对人乳头瘤病毒及其疫苗接种的认知及在西西里岛西部卫生局(阿格里真托、巴勒莫和特拉帕尼)学生群体中接受人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种的意愿

03 | Knowledge about human papillomavirus and its vaccination and willingness to accept human papillomavirus vaccination in a population of students from the Local Health Authorities of Agrigento, Palermo and Trapani, Western Sicily
作者： C. Amodeo, F. Cacioppo, N. Bonaccorso, M. Sciortino, E. Zarcone, M. Tocco, L. Pipitone, A. Carubia, L. D'Azzo, G. Bosco
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是最常见的性传播感染之一,与多种疾病的发生有关,包括宫颈癌、肛门癌、外阴癌、阴道阴茎癌、口咽癌等恶性肿瘤和尖锐湿疣等良性病变。HPV 疫苗接种是一种高效、安全的一级预防干预措施;然而,疫苗接种 计划的采用很大程度上受到目标人群(尤其是青少年)的知识水平和认知水平的影响。在这种情况下,学校的教育干预在提高人们对预防行为的认识和良好态度方面发挥着核心作用。材料和方法。该研究以西西里岛西部 3 个省份阿格里真托、巴勒莫和特拉帕尼的 2,146 名一年级和二年级学生为样本。参与者通过填写结构化问卷参与参与,该问卷旨在评估他们的知识、对 HPV 及其 疫苗接种 的态度以及接受 疫苗接种 的意愿。在旨在提供有关感染和预防策略的科学信息的培训课程后重新进行了第二版调查问卷。使用李克特量表收集回复,范围从 1 到 5 来评估意见,使用 1 到 10 来衡量 疫苗接种 意愿。参与这项研究需要学生家长事先签署的书面知情同意书。学生们对病毒可导致严重疾病的说法高度认同,平均分为 4.5 分(满分 5 分),并承认一生中接触感染的可能性,平均分为 3.9 分。家庭、学校和医疗保健环境中也出现了对该主题沟通重要性的强烈认识,平均分分别为 4.23、4.46 和 4.21。关于 疫苗接种,参与者对 疫苗 在预防 宫颈癌 和尖锐湿疣方面的有用性(平均值 4.5)、其安全性(平均值 4.32)以及同伴接种疫苗的适当性(平均值 4.48)表示高度一致。疫苗接种 意愿(按 1 到 10 的等级衡量)达到平均值 8.94,较干预前得分 8.62 有所提高(p 值)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是一种常见的性传播感染,与多种疾病相关,包括癌症和良性病变,强调需要了解其影响以及 疫苗接种 在特定人群中的意愿。
设计与对象
本研究在西西里岛西部三个省份(阿格里真托、巴勒莫和特拉帕尼)开展,涉及2,146名就读于初中和高中的学生。
核心方法
参与者完成了一份结构化调查问卷,以评估他们接受 HPV 疫苗接种 的知识、态度和意愿,并在培训课程结束后进行第二个版本以提供科学信息。
主要结果
学生们高度同意 HPV 可导致严重疾病的说法(平均分 4.5),并承认终生暴露风险(平均分 3.9)。疫苗接种 意愿在 1-10 等级上从 8.62 增加到 8.94(p 值 < 0.05)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,教育干预可以增强学生对 HPV 疫苗接种 的理解和接受意愿,特别是关于其在预防 宫颈癌 和尖锐湿疣方面的作用。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,学校有针对性的教育工作可以提高 HPV 疫苗接种 的吸收率,有助于制定旨在减少 HPV 相关疾病的公共卫生战略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单一人群,并依赖于自我报告的数据,这可能会引入偏差。然而,由于使用了结构化问卷和对结果的统计分析,证据是强有力的。

03 | Knowledge about human papillomavirus and its vaccination and willingness to accept human papillomavirus vaccination in a population of students from the Local Health Authorities of Agrigento, Palermo and Trapani, Western Sicily

Authors: C. Amodeo, F. Cacioppo, N. Bonaccorso, M. Sciortino, E. Zarcone, M. Tocco, L. Pipitone, A. Carubia, L. D'Azzo, G. Bosco
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is one of the most common sexually transmitted infection and is associated with the development of several diseases, including malignancies such as cervical, anal, vulvar, vaginal penile, oropharyngeal cancers and benign lesions such as genital warts. HPV vaccination is a highly effective and safe primary prevention intervention; however, uptake of vaccination programs is strongly influenced by the level of knowledge and perceptions of the target population, particularly among adolescents. In this context, educational interventions at schools play a central role in promoting awareness and favorable attitudes toward preventive behaviors. Materials and Methods. The study involved a sample of 2,146 students attending first and second grade secondary schools in the 3 Wester Sicily provinces of Agrigento, Palermo and Trapani. Participants were involved via the administration of a structured questionnaire designed to assess their knowledge, attitudes toward HPV and its vaccination, and willingness to receive vaccination. A second version of the questionnaire was re-administered following training sessions aimed to providing scientific information on the infection and prevention strategies. Responses were collected using Likert scales ranging from 1 to 5 to assess opinions and from 1 to 10 to measure vaccination willingness. Participation in the study required a written informed consent previously signed from the students' parents. Students showed a high level of agreement with the statement that the virus can cause serious illness, with an average score of 4.5 out of 5, and acknowledged the possibility of being exposed to the infection during their lifetime, with an average score of 3.9. A strong perception of the importance of communication on the topic also emerged, both within the family, school, and healthcare settings, with average scores of 4.23, 4.46, and 4.21, respectively. Regarding the Vaccination, participants expressed a high level of agreement regarding the vaccine's usefulness in preventing cervical cancer and genital warts (mean 4.5), its safety (mean 4.32), and the appropriateness of their peers getting vaccinated (mean 4.48). Vaccination willingness, measured on a scale of 1 to 10, reached a mean value of 8.94, an increase from the pre-intervention score of 8.62 (p-value
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a common sexually transmitted infection linked to various diseases, including cancers and benign lesions, highlighting the need for understanding its impact and vaccination willingness in a specific population.
Design and population
The study was conducted in three Western Sicily provinces (Agrigento, Palermo, and Trapani) involving 2,146 students from first and second grade secondary schools.
Core methods
Participants completed a structured questionnaire to assess their knowledge, attitudes, and willingness to accept HPV vaccination, with a second version administered after training sessions to provide scientific information.
Main results
Students showed high agreement with the statement that HPV can cause serious illness (mean score 4.5) and acknowledged lifetime exposure risk (mean score 3.9). Vaccination willingness increased from 8.62 to 8.94 on a 1-10 scale (p-value < 0.05).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that educational interventions can enhance students' understanding and willingness to accept HPV vaccination, particularly regarding its role in preventing cervical cancer and genital warts.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that targeted educational efforts in schools could improve HPV vaccination uptake, contributing to public health strategies aimed at reducing HPV-related diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single population and relies on self-reported data, which may introduce bias. However, the evidence is strong due to the use of structured questionnaires and statistical analysis of the results.

在感染HIV的男男性行为者中通过GeneXpert检测自采肛拭子中的高危型HPV:一项横断面研究的见解

High-risk HPV detection via GeneXpert in self-collected anal swabs among MSM living with HIV: Insights from a cross-sectional study
作者： Amadou Kodio, Almahady Ag Altini, Arè Albert Somboro, Aliou Baldé, Oumar Karembe, Sidi Dienta, Zalihatou Maiga, Oumar Dolo, Fatoumata Tata Traoré, Jane Holl
摘要中文翻译背景:感染HIV的男男性行为者(MSMLWHIV)面临持续高危型HPV感染的高风险,而Xpert HPV等快速工具有助于在资源匮乏地区扩大筛查范围。目的:本研究旨在评估在MSMLWHIV中使用自采肛拭子检测高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)感染的可行性,采用Xpert® HPV检测试剂盒。此外,我们还探讨了与HR-HPV阳性相关的因素。设计:这是一项单中心横断面研究,于2024年5月22日至2024年7月31日期间在马里巴马科的MSMLWHIV中开展。方法:参与者从马里巴马科Soutoura非政府组织招募。符合条件的个体需满足以下条件:HIV-1阳性、正在接受抗反转录病毒治疗、年满18岁且自认是MSM。HR-HPV的患病率按社会人口学特征和性行为进行报告。我们使用Firth方法的惩罚性逻辑回归模型识别与HR-HPV阳性相关的社会人口学和行为因素。结果:初始检测的MSMLWHIV共245人。其中170份样本检测成功,随后纳入研究分析。参与者的中位年龄为26岁(IQR 24-29),首次肛交的中位年龄为16岁(IQR 16-18)。在参与者中,29%(49/170)自认是双性恋,42%(72/170)报告从未使用安全套,16%(27/170)参与过群交,42%(71/170)在过去6个月内至少有两名不同的性伴侣。在成功检测的样本中,65%(110/170)HR-HPV阳性,其中12%(21/170)检测出HPV16。未接受或仅接受小学教育以及持续使用安全套与HR-HPV阳性风险降低相关,而吸烟的意外关联需谨慎解释。结论:MSMLWHIV中高HR-HPV 患病率的现状要求针对性筛查和预防。尽管Xpert HPV显示出潜力,但自采样本31%的失败率凸显了在样本采集程序和技术性能方面需要改进。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
感染HIV的男男性行为者(MSMLWHIV)面临持续高危型HPV感染的高风险,而Xpert HPV等快速工具有助于在资源匮乏地区扩大筛查范围。
设计与对象
这是一项单中心横断面研究,于 2024 年 5 月 22 日至 2024 年 7 月 31 日在巴马科 MSMLWHIV 患者中进行。
核心方法
参与者是从马里巴马科的 Soutoura 非政府组织招募的。符合资格的个人必须是 HIV-1 阳性、正在接受抗逆转录病毒治疗、年满 18 岁、并且自我认定为 MSM。我们使用 Firth 方法的惩罚逻辑回归模型来识别与 HR-HPV 阳性相关的社会人口统计学和行为因素。
主要结果
最初测试了总共 245 名 MSMLWHIV。其中,170 个取得了成功的结果,随后被纳入研究分析。在成功检测的参与者中,65% (110/170) 的 HR-HPV 呈阳性,其中 12% (21/170) 的参与者检测到 HPV16。未受过教育或接受过初等教育以及持续使用安全套与 HR-HPV 阳性的几率较低有关,而观察到的与吸烟的意外关联应谨慎解释。
解释与创新
MSMLWHIV 中 HR-HPV 患病率 较高,呼吁有针对性的筛查和预防。尽管 Xpert HPV 显示出前景,但自行采集样本 31% 的失败率凸显了样本采集程序和技术性能需要改进。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了针对 MSMLWHIV 中 HR-HPV 进行针对性筛查的重要性,并表明需要改进样本采集方法,以提高自我采集肛门拭子用于 HPV 检测的可靠性。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在单个中心进行的,这可能会限制普遍性。自行采集样本的失败率为 31%,表明 Xpert HPV 检测在该人群中的技术性能存在潜在局限性。

High-risk HPV detection via GeneXpert in self-collected anal swabs among MSM living with HIV: Insights from a cross-sectional study

Authors: Amadou Kodio, Almahady Ag Altini, Arè Albert Somboro, Aliou Baldé, Oumar Karembe, Sidi Dienta, Zalihatou Maiga, Oumar Dolo, Fatoumata Tata Traoré, Jane Holl
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Men who have sex with men living with HIV (MSMLWHIV) have a high risk of persistent high-risk HPV infection, and rapid tools like Xpert HPV can help expand screening in low-resource settings. OBJECTIVES: This study aimed to assess the feasibility of using self-collected anal swabs for detecting high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infections in MSMLWHIV, using the Xpert® HPV assay. Additionally, we investigated factors associated with HR-HPV positivity. DESIGN: This was a single-center cross-sectional study conducted from 22nd May 2024 to 31st July 2024 among MSMLWHIV in Bamako. METHODS: Participants were recruited from the Soutoura NGO in Bamako, Mali. To be eligible, individuals had to be HIV-1 positive, on antiretroviral therapy, 18 years or older, and self-identified as MSM. The prevalence of HR-HPV was reported by sociodemographic characteristics and sexual behaviors. We used a penalized logistic regression model using Firth's method to identify sociodemographic and behavioral factors associated with HR-HPV positivity. RESULTS: A total of 245 MSMLWHIV were initially tested. Of these, 170 yielded successful results and were subsequently included in the study analysis. The participants had a median age of 26 years (IQR 24-29) with the median age of first anal intercourse at 16 years (IQR 16-18). Among the participants, 29% (49/170) identified as bisexual, 42% (72/170) reported never using condoms, 16% (27/170) participated in group intercourse, and 42% (71/170) had at least two different sexual partners in the past six months. Among those successfully tested, 65% (110/170) were positive for HR-HPV, with HPV16 detected in 12% (21/170) of participants. No or primary education and consistent condom use were associated with lower odds of HR-HPV positivity, while the unexpected association observed with smoking should be interpreted with caution. CONCLUSION: The high HR-HPV prevalence among MSMLWHIV calls for targeted screening and prevention. Although Xpert HPV shows promise, the 31% failure rate with self-collected samples underscores the need for improvements in sample collection procedures and technical performance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Men who have sex with men living with HIV (MSMLWHIV) have a high risk of persistent high-risk HPV infection, and rapid tools like Xpert HPV can help expand screening in low-resource settings.
Design and population
This was a single-center cross-sectional study conducted from 22nd May 2024 to 31st July 2024 among MSMLWHIV in Bamako.
Core methods
Participants were recruited from the Soutoura NGO in Bamako, Mali. To be eligible, individuals had to be HIV-1 positive, on antiretroviral therapy, 18 years or older, and self-identified as MSM. We used a penalized logistic regression model using Firth's method to identify sociodemographic and behavioral factors associated with HR-HPV positivity.
Main results
A total of 245 MSMLWHIV were initially tested. Of these, 170 yielded successful results and were subsequently included in the study analysis. Among those successfully tested, 65% (110/170) were positive for HR-HPV, with HPV16 detected in 12% (21/170) of participants. No or primary education and consistent condom use were associated with lower odds of HR-HPV positivity, while the unexpected association observed with smoking should be interpreted with caution.
Interpretation and novelty
The high HR-HPV prevalence among MSMLWHIV calls for targeted screening and prevention. Although Xpert HPV shows promise, the 31% failure rate with self-collected samples underscores the need for improvements in sample collection procedures and technical performance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of targeted screening for HR-HPV in MSMLWHIV and suggest the need for improved sample collection methods to enhance the reliability of self-collected anal swabs for HPV detection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted at a single center, which may limit generalisability. The 31% failure rate with self-collected samples indicates potential limitations in the technical performance of the Xpert HPV assay in this population.

人乳头瘤病毒与肝素结合的原子分辨率结构

Atomic resolution structure of human papillomavirus bound to heparin
作者： Caroline H. Langley, Daniel J. Goetschius, Santiago Antolínez, Ebere Precious Orji, Carol M. Bator, Sarah A. Brendle, Neil D. Christensen, Jodi A. Hadden-Perilla, Susan L. Hafenstein, Jodi A. Hadden‐Perilla
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒(人乳头瘤病毒,HPV)是一项重大的公共卫生负担,也是病毒诱发癌症的主要原因。尽管已知硫酸乙酰肝素蛋白聚糖(HSPGs)介导HPV的入侵,但HPV受体结合与宿主入侵的机制尚未完全阐明。将HPV16拟病毒(由L1和L2衣壳蛋白与包装的棉尾兔乳头瘤病毒基因组组成)与肝素孵育后,该复合物被玻璃化并收集冷冻电子显微镜(cryoEM)单颗粒分析数据。提取亚颗粒并分别精修六聚体与五聚体衣壳粒。本研究呈现了1.9 Å分辨率的结构,其中肝素在五倍体顶点处的衣壳粒周围可视化。不对称单位模型被明确构建至原子分辨率cryoEM图谱中。预测L1 N端区域间存在氢键,并由分子动力学支持。确定了肝素结合位点,并观察到局部L1构象变化与全局柔性变化。肝素结合诱导的这些变化可能反映了HPV在入侵早期阶段的结构,并为未来HPV生物化学、遗传学与生物物理学研究提供框架。人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)是一项重大的公共卫生负担,但HPV受体结合机制尚未明确。本研究通过HPV与肝素结合的1.9 Å分辨率结构解析,揭示了结合界面的细节。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 受体结合和 宿主 进入机制的不完全理解,特别是硫酸乙酰肝素蛋白聚糖 (HSPG) 在介导进入中的作用。
设计与对象
作者使用由 L1 和 L2 衣壳蛋白组成的 HPV16 准病毒与包装的棉尾兔乳头瘤病毒 基因组,与肝素一起孵育,并通过冷冻电镜单颗粒分析进行分析。
核心方法
将HPV16拟病毒与肝素孵育后玻璃化,并通过冷冻电子显微镜单颗粒分析获得1.9 Å分辨率结构。提取亚颗粒并分别精修六聚体与五聚体衣壳粒。
主要结果
由此产生的 1.9 Å 分辨率结构可视化了二十面体五重顶点处的壳粒周围结合的肝素,并将不对称单元的模型明确地构建到原子分辨率冷冻电镜图中。
解释与创新
确定了肝素结合位点,以及局部 L1 构象变化和整体灵活性变化,可能反映了 HPV 在进入早期阶段的结构,并为未来的研究提供了框架。
科研与公卫意义
与肝素结合的 HPV 的原子分辨率结构促进了对 HPV 进入机制的理解,为未来与 HPV 生物学和潜在治疗靶向相关的生化、遗传和生物物理研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于体外HPV16拟病毒与肝素结合的结构分析,证据强度较高(因达到原子分辨率),但受限于缺乏体内验证。

Atomic resolution structure of human papillomavirus bound to heparin

Authors: Caroline H. Langley, Daniel J. Goetschius, Santiago Antolínez, Ebere Precious Orji, Carol M. Bator, Sarah A. Brendle, Neil D. Christensen, Jodi A. Hadden-Perilla, Susan L. Hafenstein, Jodi A. Hadden‐Perilla
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a significant health burden and leading cause of virus-induced cancers. The mechanisms of HPV receptor binding and host entry are not completely understood, although it is known that heparan sulfate proteoglycans (HSPGs) mediate entry. HPV16 quasivirus, composed of L1 and L2 capsid proteins with a packaged cottontail rabbit papillomavirus genome was incubated with heparin. The complex was vitrified, and data were collected for cryoEM single particle analysis. Subparticles were extracted and hexavalent and pentavalent capsomers were refined separately. Here we present the resulting 1.9 Å resolution structure, with heparin visualized around the capsomer at the icosahedral fivefold vertex. A model of the asymmetric unit was built unambiguously into the atomic resolution cryoEM map. Hydrogen bonds are predicted between L1 N-terminal regions, which are supported by molecular dynamics. The heparin binding site was identified, along with local L1 conformational changes and global flexibility changes. These changes induced by heparin binding likely reflect the structure of HPV during early stages of entry and provide a framework for future HPV biochemical, genetic, and biophysical studies. Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a significant health burden, but the mechanisms of HPV receptor binding are not understood. Here, the authors solve a 1.9 Å resolution structure of HPV bound to heparin with insights into the binding interface.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the incomplete understanding of human papillomavirus (HPV) receptor binding and host entry mechanisms, particularly the role of heparan sulfate proteoglycans (HSPGs) in mediating entry.
Design and population
The authors used HPV16 quasivirus composed of L1 and L2 capsid proteins with a packaged cottontail rabbit papillomavirus genome, incubated with heparin, and analyzed by cryoEM single particle analysis.
Core methods
HPV16 quasivirus was incubated with heparin, vitrified, and subjected to cryoEM single particle analysis to obtain a 1.9 Å resolution structure, with subparticles extracted and capsomers refined separately.
Main results
The resulting 1.9 Å resolution structure visualized heparin bound around the capsomer at the icosahedral fivefold vertex, with a model of the asymmetric unit built unambiguously into the atomic resolution cryoEM map.
Interpretation and novelty
The heparin binding site was identified, along with local L1 conformational changes and global flexibility changes, likely reflecting the structure of HPV during early stages of entry and providing a framework for future studies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The atomic-resolution structure of HPV bound to heparin advances mechanistic understanding of HPV entry, informing future biochemical, genetic, and biophysical studies relevant to HPV biology and potential therapeutic targeting.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to structural analysis of HPV16 quasivirus in vitro with heparin, and the evidence strength is high due to the atomic resolution achieved but remains constrained by the absence of in vivo validation.

一种细胞穿透肽与信号肽融合DNA 疫苗可增强针对HPV-16 宫颈癌抗原的CD8+ T细胞介导的抗肿瘤免疫

A Cell-Penetrating Peptide and Signal Sequence Fusion DNA Vaccine Enhances CD8+ T Cell-Mediated Anti-Tumor Immunity Targeting HPV-16 Cervical Cancer Antigens.
作者： Yu-Cheng Chang, Ky McSweeney, Yichu Xu, Yi-Ning Liu, Y. Tsai, T. Wu, Chien-Fu Hung
摘要中文翻译背景 宫颈癌是女性第四常见癌症,几乎所有病例均由感染人乳头瘤病毒(人乳头瘤病毒,HPV)引起。早期研究已探索细胞穿透肽在提升肽疫苗疗效及信号肽在增强DNA 疫苗蛋白分泌中的潜力。本研究评估了靶向HPV-16 E6和E7病毒抗原的DNA 疫苗在体内疗效,该DNA构建了细胞穿透肽与IL-2信号肽融合以提高免疫原性。结果 我们构建了多种DNA 疫苗载体,包括E7(49-57)、E6(48-57)、IL-2信号序列(sig)、9-聚精氨酸(9R)及12-聚精氨酸(12R),并将其克隆至pcDNA3 媒介载体。转染pcDNA3-sig9RE7、pcDNA3-sig12RE7或pcDNA3-9RE7 疫苗载体后,体外可诱导显著的E7特异性CD8+ T细胞应答。研究发现,与pcDNA3-9RE7及pcDNA3-sig12RE7载体相比,pcDNA3-sig9RE7 疫苗在体内诱导了最强的E7特异性免疫应答。我们证明了pcDNA3-sig9RE7 疫苗在小鼠HPV-16 E7表达TC-1肿瘤模型中具有潜在的保护性及治疗性抗肿瘤效应。最后,我们发现pcDNA3-sig9RE6 疫苗同样具有类似的抗肿瘤效应,提示该DNA 疫苗策略可应用于多种宫颈癌抗原。结论 治疗与pcDNA3-sig9RE7 DNA 疫苗在小鼠体内可诱导强效E7特异性抗肿瘤免疫应答。通过展示细胞穿透肽与信号肽序列联合应用以提升疗效的潜力,本研究为未来融合DNA 疫苗与细胞穿透肽及信号肽组合的研究奠定了基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 是影响女性的第四大常见癌症,几乎所有病例都是由 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染引起。先前的研究探索了细胞穿透肽提高肽 疫苗 功效的潜力,以及信号肽序列增加 DNA 疫苗 中蛋白质分泌的潜力。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,我们评估了 DNA 疫苗 靶向 HPV-16 E6 和 E7 病毒抗原的体内功效,这些抗原由细胞穿透肽和 IL-2 信号肽构建,以提高免疫原性。
核心方法
我们利用克隆到 pcDNA3 媒介 中的 E7(49-57)、E6(48-57)、IL-2 信号序列 (sig)、9-聚精氨酸 (9R) 和 12-聚精氨酸 (12R) 的组合生成 DNA 疫苗 构建体。
主要结果
pcDNA3-sig9RE7、pcDNA3-sig12RE7 或 pcDNA3-9RE7 疫苗 构建体的转染在体外诱导显着的 E7 特异性 CD8+ T 细胞反应。我们观察到,与 pcDNA3-9RE7 和 pcDNA3-sig12RE7 构建体相比,pcDNA3-sig9RE7 疫苗 在体内诱导最强的 E7 特异性 免疫应答。我们证明了 pcDNA3-sig9RE7 疫苗接种 对小鼠中表达 HPV-16 E7 的 TC-1 肿瘤模型的潜在保护和治疗性抗肿瘤作用。最后,我们发现 pcDNA3-sig9RE6 疫苗 具有类似的抗肿瘤作用,这表明该 DNA 疫苗 策略可以应用于各种 宫颈癌 抗原。
解释与创新
治疗与pcDNA3-sig9RE7 DNA 疫苗在小鼠体内可诱导强效E7特异性抗肿瘤免疫应答。通过展示细胞穿透肽与信号肽序列联合应用以提升疗效的潜力,本研究为未来融合DNA 疫苗与细胞穿透肽及信号肽组合的研究奠定了基础。
科研与公卫意义
本研究为融合DNA 疫苗与细胞穿透肽及信号肽组合的未来研究奠定了基础,该策略有望增强针对HPV相关宫颈癌的免疫原性及抗肿瘤免疫。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告本研究的局限性。

A Cell-Penetrating Peptide and Signal Sequence Fusion DNA Vaccine Enhances CD8+ T Cell-Mediated Anti-Tumor Immunity Targeting HPV-16 Cervical Cancer Antigens.

Authors: Yu-Cheng Chang, Ky McSweeney, Yichu Xu, Yi-Ning Liu, Y. Tsai, T. Wu, Chien-Fu Hung
Original abstract (English)
Background Cervical cancer is the fourth most common cancer affecting women, with almost all cases caused by infection with Human Papillomavirus (HPV). Previous research has explored the potential of cell-penetrating peptides to improve the efficacy of peptide vaccines and signal peptide sequences to increase protein secretion in DNA vaccines. In this study, we evaluated the in vivo efficacy of DNA vaccines targeting HPV-16 E6 and E7 viral antigens that are constructed with both a cell-penetrating peptide and IL-2 signal peptide to improve immunogenicity. Results We generated DNA vaccine constructs utilizing combinations of E7(49-57), E6(48-57), IL-2 signal sequence (sig), 9-polyarginine (9R), and 12-polyarginine (12R) cloned into pcDNA3 vectors. Transfection of the pcDNA3-sig9RE7, pcDNA3-sig12RE7, or pcDNA3-9RE7 vaccine constructs induced a significant E7-specific CD8+ T cell response in vitro. We observed that the pcDNA3-sig9RE7 vaccine induced the strongest E7-specific immune response in vivo compared to the pcDNA3-9RE7 and pcDNA3-sig12RE7 constructs. We demonstrated the potential protective and therapeutic anti-tumor effects of the pcDNA3-sig9RE7 vaccination against the HPV-16 E7 expressing TC-1 tumor model in mice. Finally, we found similar anti-tumor effects for the pcDNA3-sig9RE6 vaccine which suggest this DNA vaccine strategy could be applied to various cervical cancer antigens. Conclusion Treatment with the pcDNA3-sig9RE7 DNA vaccine induces a potent E7-specific anti-tumor immune response in mice. By demonstrating the potential of utilizing a cell-penetrating peptide and signal peptide sequence in combination to improve efficacy, this study provides a foundation for future research into fusion DNA vaccines with cell-penetrating peptides and signal peptide combinations.
View research seven-element analysis

Scientific and public-health significance
This study provides a foundation for future research into fusion DNA vaccines with cell-penetrating peptides and signal peptide combinations, which could enhance immunogenicity and anti-tumor immunity against HPV-associated cervical cancer.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.

用于检测 HPV-16 DNA 的蒽基锌框架的共反应物特异性电化学发光。

Coreactant-specific electrochemiluminescence of an anthracene-based zinc framework for detection of HPV-16 DNA.
作者： Zheng Tao, Yufei Wang, Xueyi Zheng, Zhiyuan Xu, Jianping Lei
摘要中文翻译共反应物的有效激活在电化学发光(ECL)增强中具有挑战性。在此,设计了一种以蒽衍生物作为发光体配体和Zn 2+ 节点的金属有机骨架纳米发射体。它通过共反应物和 Zn 2+ 节点之间的协调相互作用证明了 1,4-二氮杂双环[2.2.2]辛烷 (DABCO) 特异性 ECL 途径,与传统共反应物相比,显着增强了发射,建立了 DNA 检测的灵敏策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决在电化学发光(ECL)增强中高效激活共反应物的挑战,特别是用于HPV-16 DNA的检测。
设计与对象
该研究的重点是开发一种金属有机框架纳米发射器,以蒽衍生物作为发光体配体和Zn 2+ 节点,专门用于检测HPV-16 DNA。
核心方法
该方法涉及设计一种以蒽衍生物为发光体配体和Zn 2+ 节点的金属有机骨架纳米发射体,并通过共反应物与Zn 2+ 节点之间的配位相互作用展示其ECL途径。
主要结果
该纳米发射器展示了 1,4-二氮杂双环[2.2.2]辛烷 (DABCO) 特异性 ECL 途径,与传统共反应物相比显着增强了发射,从而建立了 DNA 检测的灵敏策略。
解释与创新
作者将这一发现解释为一种新颖且灵敏的 DNA 检测策略,利用了所设计的纳米发射器的特定 ECL 途径。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为开发更灵敏、高效的HPV-16 DNA检测方法提供依据,这对于HPV相关疾病的早期诊断与预防至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于在受控环境中展示 ECL 通路,并且证据是强有力的,因为它基于直接实验结果。

Coreactant-specific electrochemiluminescence of an anthracene-based zinc framework for detection of HPV-16 DNA.

Authors: Zheng Tao, Yufei Wang, Xueyi Zheng, Zhiyuan Xu, Jianping Lei
Original abstract (English)
The efficient activation of a coreactant is challenging in electrochemiluminescence (ECL) enhancement. Herein, a metal-organic framework nanoemitter was designed with an anthracene derivative as a luminophore ligand and a Zn 2+ node. It demonstrated a 1,4-diazabicyclo[2.2.2]octane (DABCO)-specific ECL pathway via coordination interaction between the coreactant and the Zn 2+ node, which significantly enhanced emission over conventional coreactants, establishing a sensitive strategy for DNA detection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of efficiently activating a coreactant in electrochemiluminescence (ECL) enhancement, particularly for the detection of HPV-16 DNA.
Design and population
The study focused on the development of a metal-organic framework nanoemitter with an anthracene derivative as a luminophore ligand and a Zn 2+ node, specifically for the detection of HPV-16 DNA.
Core methods
The method involved designing a metal-organic framework nanoemitter with an anthracene derivative as a luminophore ligand and a Zn 2+ node, and demonstrating its ECL pathway through coordination interaction between the coreactant and the Zn 2+ node.
Main results
The nanoemitter demonstrated a 1,4-diazabicyclo[2.2.2]octane (DABCO)-specific ECL pathway, significantly enhancing emission over conventional coreactants, which established a sensitive strategy for DNA detection.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a novel and sensitive strategy for DNA detection, leveraging the specific ECL pathway of the designed nanoemitter.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more sensitive and efficient DNA detection methods for HPV-16, which is critical for early diagnosis and prevention of HPV-associated diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to demonstrating the ECL pathway in a controlled setting, and the evidence is strong as it is based on direct experimental results.

宫颈上皮内瘤变后长期癌症风险:一项利用台湾国家健康保险研究数据库的重要研究

Long-term risk of cancer following cervical intraepithelial neoplasia: A significant study leveraging Taiwan's national health insurance research database
作者： Yi‐Hung Sun, Jing‐Yang Huang, En-Shu Kuo, Shun‐Fa Yang
摘要中文翻译目的:我们利用台湾国民健康保险研究数据库进行宫颈上皮内瘤变后癌症风险的研究。材料和方法:该研究分析了台湾 NHIRD 的数据,比较了三种 队列研究:一种患有癌前宫颈不典型增生(CIN2+ 不典型增生),一种患有轻度不典型增生或未确定的不典型增生(非 CIN2+ 不典型增生),以及一种无不典型增生。我们排除了索引日期在 2001 年之前或 2016 年之后的患者,并调整了因素来分析未来的癌症风险。结果:CIN2+ 队列研究、非 CIN2+ 队列研究 和无宫颈发育不良的 队列研究 中分别有 776、1248 和 14,421 例新发病例。CIN2+ 和非 CIN2+ 队列研究 中所有癌症、宫颈癌或宫颈内膜癌、肛门生殖道恶性肿瘤、头颈癌、呼吸道癌、乳腺癌、卵巢癌或子宫癌以及结肠癌风险的竞争风险比 (95% CI) 分别为 2.312 (2.112-2.53) 和 1.195 (1.104-1.293)、15.518 (11.936-20.175) 和 6.045 (4.713-7.754)、9.369 (4.022-21.821) 和 4.451 (2.01-9.856)、2.328 (1.446-3.746) 和1.111 (0.707-1.746)、2.125 (1.622-2.783) 和 1.147 (0.896-1.467)、1.794 (1.492-2.158) 和 0.98 (0.843-1.139)、0.786 (0.45-1.372) 和分别为 1.375 (1.055-1.793)、2.315 (1.832-2.927) 和 1.114 (0.885-1.401)。结论:这项研究表明,中度或重度宫颈上皮内瘤变和原位癌会显着增加长期癌症风险,即使在手术干预后也是如此。有轻度或未确定的宫颈发育不良病史的患者也面临着较高的癌症风险,强​​调需要持续监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查宫颈上皮内瘤变后患癌症的长期风险,解决了解癌前病变与后续恶性肿瘤之间关系的需要。
设计与对象
该研究利用台湾国民健康保险研究数据库,比较了三名队列研究:癌前宫颈不典型增生(CIN2+不典型增生)、轻度不典型增生或未确定不典型增生(非CIN2+不典型增生)和非不典型增生。该分析分别包括 队列研究 中的 776 个、1248 个和 14,421 个新病例。
核心方法
该研究排除了索引日期在 2001 年之前或 2016 年之后的患者,并根据各种因素进行调整以分析未来的癌症风险。它使用竞争风险比来评估 队列研究 中不同癌症类型的风险。
主要结果
CIN2+ 队列研究 的癌症风险竞争风险比 (95% CI) 为 2.312 (2.112-2.53),非 CIN2+ 队列研究 的竞争风险比 (95% CI) 为 1.195 (1.104-1.293),在几种癌症类型中观察到显着差异。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,中度或重度宫颈上皮内瘤变和原位癌会显着增加长期癌症风险,即使在手术干预后也是如此,这凸显了持续监测的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,需要对宫颈上皮内瘤变患者进行一致的 监测 和管理,以减轻长期癌症风险,特别是与 ZX​​QTERMFQXZ 相关的恶性肿瘤。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对国家健康保险数据库的依赖而受到限制,该数据库可能存在偏差或缺失数据,并且由于分析的观察性质,证据并不充分。

Long-term risk of cancer following cervical intraepithelial neoplasia: A significant study leveraging Taiwan's national health insurance research database

Authors: Yi‐Hung Sun, Jing‐Yang Huang, En-Shu Kuo, Shun‐Fa Yang
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: We used Taiwan's national health insurance research database to conduct a study on the risk of cancer after cervical intraepithelial neoplasia. MATERIALS AND METHODS: The study analyzed data from the Taiwan NHIRD comparing three cohorts: one with precancerous cervical dysplasia (CIN2+ dysplasia), one with mild dysplasia or undetermined dysplasia (non-CIN2+ dysplasia), and one without dysplasia. We excluded patients with index dates before 2001 or after 2016 and adjusted factors to analyze future cancer risks. RESULTS: There were 776, 1248, and 14,421 new cases in the CIN2+ cohort, non-CIN2+ cohort, and cohort without cervical dysplasia, respectively. The competing hazard ratios (95% CI) for the risk of all cancer, cervical or endocervical cancer, malignancy of the anogenital tract, head and neck cancer, respiratory cancer, breast cancer, ovary or uterus cancer, and colon cancer in the CIN2+ and non-CIN2+ cohorts were 2.312 (2.112-2.53) and 1.195 (1.104-1.293), 15.518 (11.936-20.175) and 6.045 (4.713-7.754), 9.369 (4.022-21.821) and 4.451 (2.01-9.856), 2.328 (1.446-3.746) and 1.111 (0.707-1.746), 2.125 (1.622-2.783) and 1.147 (0.896-1.467), 1.794 (1.492-2.158) and 0.98 (0.843-1.139), 0.786 (0.45-1.372) and 1.375 (1.055-1.793), 2.315 (1.832-2.927) and 1.114 (0.885-1.401), respectively. CONCLUSION: This study demonstrates that moderate or severe cervical intraepithelial neoplasia and carcinoma in situ significantly increase long-term cancer risk, even after surgical intervention. Patients with a history of mild or undetermined cervical dysplasia also face heightened cancer risk, emphasizing the need for consistent monitoring.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the long-term risk of cancer following cervical intraepithelial neoplasia, addressing the need to understand the association between precancerous lesions and subsequent malignancies.
Design and population
The study used Taiwan's national health insurance research database to compare three cohorts: those with precancerous cervical dysplasia (CIN2+ dysplasia), those with mild dysplasia or undetermined dysplasia (non-CIN2+ dysplasia), and those without dysplasia. The analysis included 776, 1248, and 14,421 new cases in these cohorts, respectively.
Core methods
The study excluded patients with index dates before 2001 or after 2016 and adjusted for various factors to analyze future cancer risks. It used competing hazard ratios to assess the risk of different cancer types across the cohorts.
Main results
The competing hazard ratios (95% CI) for cancer risk were 2.312 (2.112-2.53) for the CIN2+ cohort and 1.195 (1.104-1.293) for the non-CIN2+ cohort, with significant differences observed for several cancer types.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that moderate or severe cervical intraepithelial neoplasia and carcinoma in situ significantly increase long-term cancer risk, even after surgical intervention, highlighting the importance of ongoing monitoring.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the need for consistent surveillance and management of patients with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia to mitigate long-term cancer risks, particularly for high-risk HPV-related malignancies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a national health insurance database, which may have biases or missing data, and the evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the analysis.

Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the prevalence and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus (HPV) in primary lung cancer patients in Eastern India, addressing the knowledge gap regarding HPV's role in lung cancer.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study was conducted in Eastern India between 2022 and 2024, involving 150 patients diagnosed with primary lung cancer.
Core methods
HPV DNA was detected using polymerase chain reaction (PCR) and genotyping was performed through sequencing to identify specific HPV types present in the lung cancer specimens.

宫颈上皮内瘤变2级(CIN2)锥切术后的临床病理特征及长期结局

Clinicopathological Characteristics and Long-Term Outcomes After Conization for Cervical Intraepithelial Neoplasia Grade 2 (CIN2)
作者： Sevda Baş, Büşra Asena Torun, Sibel Öncel Yavuz, Oğuz Uyar, Aslıhan Duman
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估行宫颈锥切术治疗的宫颈上皮内瘤变2级(CIN2)患者的临床病理特征及长期结局,并探索与残留或复发性疾病相关的因素。
设计与对象
研究共纳入2016年7月至2026年2月期间接受治疗的195名患者。中位随访时长为1.75年(四分位距,0.83–5.04年)。
核心方法
本研究通过锥切术后组织学评估病变的降级、持续及进展情况,并评估手术切缘及随访发现以判断残留或复发性疾病。
主要结果
锥切术后组织学显示,50.7%的病例降级至CIN1或正常,35.8%持续为CIN2,13.3%进展为高级别病变。88.2%的患者手术切缘为阴性。早期随访中,26例患者出现残留病变的影像学表现,但组织学确认的残留CIN2+病变仅3例。随访期间,9例患者出现复发的影像学表现,而组织学确认的复发性CIN2+病变仅3例。
解释与创新
研究结果提示,大多数CIN2病变在锥切术后退行或保持稳定,高级别残留或复发性疾病的组织学确认率较低,并凸显常规临床病理因素在识别复发风险患者方面的有限预测价值。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为宫颈锥切术后CIN2的自然病程提供了见解,可为HPV相关宫颈疾病的临床决策及监测策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
本研究依赖组织学确认残留或复发性疾病,可能无法涵盖所有病例,证据强度为中等,因研究为观察性设计且存在潜在混杂因素。

Clinicopathological Characteristics and Long-Term Outcomes After Conization for Cervical Intraepithelial Neoplasia Grade 2 (CIN2)

Authors: Sevda Baş, Büşra Asena Torun, Sibel Öncel Yavuz, Oğuz Uyar, Aslıhan Duman
Original abstract (English)
Cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 (CIN2) represents a clinically heterogeneous condition with variable risks of persistence, regression, and progression. This study evaluated the clinicopathological characteristics and long-term outcomes of patients undergoing cervical conization for CIN2 and explored factors associated with residual or recurrent disease. A total of 195 patients treated between July 2016 and February 2026 were included. The median follow-up duration was 1.75 years (IQR, 0.83–5.04 years). Post-conization histology showed that 50.7% of cases were downgraded to CIN1 or normal, 35.8% persisted as CIN2, and 13.3% were upgraded to higher-grade lesions. Negative surgical margins were observed in 88.2% of patients. Findings suggestive of residual disease were identified in 26 patients during early follow-up, although histologically confirmed residual CIN2+ disease was observed in only three cases. During follow-up, nine patients developed findings suggestive for recurrence, whereas histologically confirmed recurrent CIN2+ disease was confirmed in three patients. HPV clearance was observed in 75% of patients with available follow-up testing. Multivariable analysis did not identify any independent predictors of relapse. These findings suggest that most CIN2 lesions regress or remain stable after conization, with low rates of histologically confirmed residual or recurrent high-grade disease, and highlight the limited predictive value of conventional clinicopathological factors in identifying patients at risk of relapse.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the clinicopathological characteristics and long-term outcomes of patients with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 (CIN2) after conization and to explore factors associated with residual or recurrent disease.
Design and population
A total of 195 patients treated between July 2016 and February 2026 were included in the study. The median follow-up duration was 1.75 years (IQR, 0.83–5.04 years).
Core methods
The study used post-conization histology to assess lesion downgrades, persistence, and upgrades. It also evaluated surgical margins and follow-up findings for residual or recurrent disease.
Main results
Post-conization histology showed that 50.7% of cases were downgraded to CIN1 or normal, 35.8% persisted as CIN2, and 13.3% were upgraded to higher-grade lesions. Negative surgical margins were observed in 88.2% of patients. Findings suggestive of residual disease were identified in 26 patients during early follow-up, although histologically confirmed residual CIN2+ disease was observed in only three cases. During follow-up, nine patients developed findings suggestive for recurrence, whereas histologically confirmed recurrent CIN2+ disease was confirmed in three patients.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that most CIN2 lesions regress or remain stable after conization, with low rates of histologically confirmed residual or recurrent high-grade disease, and highlight the limited predictive value of conventional clinicopathological factors in identifying patients at risk of relapse.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the natural history of CIN2 following conization, which can inform clinical decision-making and surveillance strategies for HPV-associated cervical disease.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on histological confirmation for residual or recurrent disease, which may not capture all cases. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study and potential for confounding factors.
