应用 Cobas 6800 HPV 检测和 qPCR,利用三种基因组优化的引物组检测 HPV 阳性不典型增生和癌症患者血液中的 HPV DNA

Application of Cobas 6800 HPV testing and qPCR utilizing three genomically-optimized primer sets for detecting HPV DNA in the blood of HPV Positive dysplasia and cancer patients
作者： Heba H. Mostafa, Amary Fall, Stéphanie L. Gaillard, Carole Fakhry, Zubair Khan, Tanguy Y Seiwert, Hao Wang, Hua-Ling Tsai, Amanda N. Fader, Charles A. Leath
摘要中文翻译使用定量聚合酶链式反应 (qPCR) 测量血浆中的病毒 DNA 有望检测和/或监测高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 相关癌症。我们检查了罗氏的 cobas 6800 HPV 检测,用于检测 HPV 相关不典型增生或癌症患者的血浆或外周血单核细胞 (PBMC) 中的 hrHPV。在我们的初步研究中,该测定在晚期(II-IV 期)宫颈癌 患者的 1 mL 治疗 前血浆中检测到 hrHPV DNA,但未在 I 期或发育不良病例或 PBMC 中检测到 hrHPV DNA。大多数口咽癌患者的血浆检测结果也呈阳性。为了提高灵敏度,我们使用 qPCR 重新测试了这些试点样品,使用了三个已发布的引物组(E7、BR E1-5、HR E5L2-4),这些引物组选择用于改进癌症中 HPV16 的检测。该三重检测检测到晚期(II-IV期)宫颈癌病例的治疗前血浆中HPV16的可定量水平,以及一些I期和发育不良病例中较低的水平。在 治疗 后约 2 年内没有疾病证据的 HPV16 + 宫颈癌 患者的连续采样中,cobas HPV 检测未检测到可重复的信号,而三重 qPCR 在这些患者的子集的血浆和 PBMC 样本中偶尔检测到 HPV DNA。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估qPCR检测血浆中HPV DNA的实用性,并探索其在监测高危型HPV相关癌症中的潜在价值。
设计与对象
一项前瞻性研究使用了来自晚期宫颈癌患者、I期宫颈癌患者、HPV相关发育不良患者以及口咽癌患者的术前治疗血浆样本。
核心方法
该研究采用罗氏的cobas 6800 HPV检测和三重qPCR以及三个基因组优化的引物组(E7、BR E1-5、HR E5L2-4)来检测血浆样本中的HPV16和其他hrHPV类型。
主要结果
cobas HPV检测在晚期宫颈癌血浆中检测到高危型HPV DNA,但在I期或发育不良病例或PBMC中未检测到。三重qPCR在晚期宫颈癌血浆中检测到可量化的HPV16水平,并在部分I期和发育不良病例中检测到较低水平。术后治疗系列采样显示cobas检测无可重复信号,但使用三重qPCR在部分患者中观察到零星HPV DNA检测。
解释与创新
与 cobas 检测相比,三重 qPCR 检测血浆中 HPV16 的灵敏度更高,这表明它有可能更准确地监测 HPV 相关癌症。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为开发更敏感的HPV相关癌症诊断工具提供参考,特别是基于血浆的检测,这有助于提升诊断和监测策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于试点样本量,并重点关注 HPV16,这可能会影响普遍性。由于该研究的探索性和缺乏长期随访数据,证据是中等的。

Application of Cobas 6800 HPV testing and qPCR utilizing three genomically-optimized primer sets for detecting HPV DNA in the blood of HPV Positive dysplasia and cancer patients

Authors: Heba H. Mostafa, Amary Fall, Stéphanie L. Gaillard, Carole Fakhry, Zubair Khan, Tanguy Y Seiwert, Hao Wang, Hua-Ling Tsai, Amanda N. Fader, Charles A. Leath
Original abstract (English)
Use of quantitative polymerase chain reaction (qPCR) to measure viral DNA in plasma has promise for the detection and/or monitoring of high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV)-associated cancers. We examined Roche’s cobas 6800 HPV test for detection of hrHPV in plasma or peripheral blood mononuclear cells (PBMC) of patients with HPV-associated dysplasia or cancer. In our pilot study, this assay detected hrHPV DNA in 1 mL pre-treatment plasma of patients with advanced (stage II-IV) cervical cancer, but not stage I or dysplasia cases, or in PBMC. Plasma of oropharyngeal cancer patients also tested positive in most cases. Seeking to enhance sensitivity, we re-tested these pilot samples with qPCR employing three published primer sets (E7, BR E1-5, HR E5L2-4) selected for improved detection of HPV16 in cancer. This triplex assay detected quantifiable levels of HPV16 in pre-treatment plasma of advanced (stage II-IV) cervical cancer cases, and lower levels in some stage I and dysplasia cases. In serial sampling of HPV16 + cervical cancer patients post-treatment with no evidence of disease over ~2 years, no reproducible signal was detected by cobas HPV testing, whereas the triplex qPCR sporadically detected HPV DNA in plasma and PBMC samples of a subset of these patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the utility of qPCR for detecting HPV DNA in plasma and to explore its potential for monitoring hrHPV-associated cancers.
Design and population
A pilot study was conducted using pre-treatment plasma samples from patients with advanced cervical cancer, stage I cervical cancer, and HPV-associated dysplasia, as well as oropharyngeal cancer patients.
Core methods
The study employed Roche’s cobas 6800 HPV test and triplex qPCR with three genomically-optimized primer sets (E7, BR E1-5, HR E5L2-4) to detect HPV16 and other hrHPV types in plasma samples.
Main results
The cobas HPV test detected hrHPV DNA in advanced cervical cancer plasma but not in stage I or dysplasia cases or PBMC. The triplex qPCR detected quantifiable HPV16 levels in advanced cervical cancer plasma and lower levels in some stage I and dysplasia cases. Post-treatment serial sampling showed no reproducible signal with cobas testing, but sporadic HPV DNA detection was observed in a subset of patients using triplex qPCR.
Interpretation and novelty
The triplex qPCR demonstrated improved sensitivity for detecting HPV16 in plasma compared to the cobas test, suggesting its potential for more accurate monitoring of HPV-associated cancers.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform the development of more sensitive diagnostic tools for HPV-associated cancers, particularly in plasma-based detection, which could enhance surveillance and monitoring strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a pilot sample size and focused on HPV16, which may affect generalizability. The evidence is moderate due to the exploratory nature of the study and the lack of long-term follow-up data.

1990-2023年印度南部宫颈癌负担的长期趋势及疫情后偏离:一项《2023年全球疾病负担》研究

Long-term trends and post-pandemic deviations in cervical cancer burden in South India, 1990-2023: a Global Burden of Disease 2023 study.
作者： Indra Sivakumar, Jeevarathinam Thirumalai, Anees Ahmed Farooq Mohammed
摘要中文翻译目标:尽管通过 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 和筛查在预防方面取得了进展,但 宫颈癌 仍然是印度的一项重大公共卫生挑战。尽管全国估计数据显示逐渐下降,但显着的地区差异仍然存在,并且与 2019 年冠状病毒病相关的破坏可能影响了近期的疾病轨迹。这项研究评估了印度南部的长期趋势、大流行后的偏差以及世界卫生组织 (WHO) 消除目标的进展情况。方法:这项基于人群的观察分析使用了《2023 年全球疾病负担研究》对 1990 年至 2023 年印度南部 30-69 岁女性的估计。评估了大流行前的趋势和反事实预测,并将年龄标准化的 发病率 率 (ASR) 与世界卫生组织每 100,000 名女性年 ≤ 4 例病例的消除阈值进行了描述性比较。结果:年龄标准化的 宫颈癌、发病率 和 死亡率 直到 2010 年代初才有所下降,但仍高于国家和全球水平。此后,跌势停滞并出现逆转。2023 年,发病率 的范围从喀拉拉邦每 10 万名女性 26.1 人到特伦甘纳邦每 10 万名女性 65.8 人;根据这些 ASR,所有州都超过了 WHO 的阈值。观察到的比率超出了反事实预测,但每个地点和结果的不确定性范围都为零。因此,研究结果与大流行后 宫颈癌 服务可能受到的破坏一致,而不是统计上证明的过度负担,并且这种生态比较无法建立因果关系。50-69 岁女性的残疾调整生命年率最高。结论:南印度最近似乎经历了 宫颈癌 控制势头的丧失。加强 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖范围、扩大筛查范围和提高癌症预防服务的弹性对于加快实现消除目标至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管通过 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 和筛查在预防方面取得了进展,但 宫颈癌 仍然是印度的一项重大公共卫生挑战。尽管全国估计数据显示逐渐下降,但显着的地区差异仍然存在,并且与 2019 年冠状病毒病相关的破坏可能影响了近期的疾病轨迹。
设计与对象
本研究评估了印度南部长期趋势、疫情后偏离及向世界卫生组织(WHO)消除目标的进展。研究基于《2023年全球疾病负担研究》估计值,分析1990-2023年印度南部30-69岁女性人群的数据。
核心方法
这项基于人群的观察分析使用了《2023 年全球疾病负担研究》对 1990 年至 2023 年印度南部 30-69 岁女性的估计。评估了大流行前的趋势和反事实预测,并将年龄标准化的 发病率 率 (ASR) 与世界卫生组织每 100,000 名女性年 ≤ 4 例病例的消除阈值进行了描述性比较。
主要结果
Age-standardized cervical cancer incidence and mortality declined until the early 2010s but remained above national and global rates. Thereafter, declines stagnated and reversed. In 2023, incidence ranged from 26.1 per 100,000 women in Kerala to 65.8 in Telangana; all states exceeded the WHO threshold based on these ASRs.
解释与创新
印度南部似乎最近经历了 宫颈癌 控制势头的丧失。研究结果与大流行后 宫颈癌 服务可能受到的破坏一致,而不是统计上证明的过度负担,而且这种生态比较无法建立因果关系。
科研与公卫意义
加强 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖范围、扩大筛查范围和提高癌症预防服务的弹性对于加快实现消除目标至关重要。50-69 岁女性的残疾调整生命年率最高。
局限与证据强度
观察到的比率超出了反事实预测,但每个地点和结果的不确定性范围都为零。提供的证据没有报告与研究设计或统计方法相关的具体限制。

Long-term trends and post-pandemic deviations in cervical cancer burden in South India, 1990-2023: a Global Burden of Disease 2023 study.

Authors: Indra Sivakumar, Jeevarathinam Thirumalai, Anees Ahmed Farooq Mohammed
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: Cervical cancer remains a major public health challenge in India despite advances in prevention through human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination and screening. Although national estimates indicate gradual declines, marked regional disparities persist, and coronavirus disease 2019-related disruptions may have affected recent disease trajectories. This study assessed long-term trends, post-pandemic deviations, and progress toward World Health Organization (WHO) elimination targets in South India. METHODS: This population-based observational analysis used Global Burden of Disease Study 2023 estimates for women aged 30-69 years in South India from 1990 to 2023. Pre-pandemic trends and counterfactual projections were evaluated, and age-standardized incidence rates (ASRs) were descriptively compared with the WHO elimination threshold of ≤4 cases per 100,000 woman-years. RESULTS: Age-standardized cervical cancer incidence and mortality declined until the early 2010s but remained above national and global rates. Thereafter, declines stagnated and reversed. In 2023, incidence ranged from 26.1 per 100,000 women in Kerala to 65.8 in Telangana; all states exceeded the WHO threshold based on these ASRs. Observed rates exceeded counterfactual projections, but uncertainty envelopes included zero for every location and outcome. Thus, the findings are consistent with possible post-pandemic disruption to cervical cancer services rather than statistically demonstrated excess burden, and this ecological comparison cannot establish causality. Disability-adjusted life-year rates were highest among women aged 50-69 years. CONCLUSION: South India appears to have experienced a recent loss of momentum in cervical cancer control. Strengthening HPV vaccination coverage, expanding screening, and improving the resilience of cancer prevention services are essential for accelerating progress toward elimination targets.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer remains a major public health challenge in India despite advances in prevention through human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination and screening. Although national estimates indicate gradual declines, marked regional disparities persist, and coronavirus disease 2019-related disruptions may have affected recent disease trajectories.
Design and population
This study assessed long-term trends, post-pandemic deviations, and progress toward World Health Organization (WHO) elimination targets in South India. It used Global Burden of Disease Study 2023 estimates for women aged 30-69 years in South India from 1990 to 2023.
Core methods
This population-based observational analysis used Global Burden of Disease Study 2023 estimates for women aged 30-69 years in South India from 1990 to 2023. Pre-pandemic trends and counterfactual projections were evaluated, and age-standardized incidence rates (ASRs) were descriptively compared with the WHO elimination threshold of ≤4 cases per 100,000 woman-years.
Main results
Age-standardized cervical cancer incidence and mortality declined until the early 2010s but remained above national and global rates. Thereafter, declines stagnated and reversed. In 2023, incidence ranged from 26.1 per 100,000 women in Kerala to 65.8 in Telangana; all states exceeded the WHO threshold based on these ASRs.
Interpretation and novelty
South India appears to have experienced a recent loss of momentum in cervical cancer control. The findings are consistent with possible post-pandemic disruption to cervical cancer services rather than statistically demonstrated excess burden, and this ecological comparison cannot establish causality.
Scientific and public-health significance
Strengthening HPV vaccination coverage, expanding screening, and improving the resilience of cancer prevention services are essential for accelerating progress toward elimination targets. Disability-adjusted life-year rates were highest among women aged 50-69 years.
Limitations and evidence strength
Observed rates exceeded counterfactual projections, but uncertainty envelopes included zero for every location and outcome. The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations related to the study design or statistical methods.

幼年发病复发性呼吸道乳头状瘤病患者在非标签适应症下接种九价人乳头瘤病毒疫苗后的免疫应答与临床过程

Immunologic Response and Clinical Course After Off-label Nonavalent HPV Vaccination in Juvenile-onset Recurrent Respiratory Papillomatosis Patients.
作者： Neele M. Thurau, Inga Tometten, Nadine Lübke, Birgitta E. Michels, Daniela Höfler, Fabian Rosing, Peter Sehr, Katharina Remke, Jörg Timm, Steffi Silling
摘要中文翻译背景:幼年发病复发性呼吸道乳头状瘤病(JoRRP)是一种由人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)引起的罕见气道疾病,最常见的病原体为HPV6或HPV11。受影响的儿童通常需要反复进行手术治疗,且目前尚无治愈性疗法。非标签适应症下的人乳头瘤病毒疫苗已被提出作为辅助治疗手段,但疫苗诱导的免疫应答及循环HPV DNA在良性疾病中的相关性仍不明确。方法:我们对2020年至2025年间接受非标签适应症九价人乳头瘤病毒疫苗接种的2名JoRRP患儿进行了纵向评估。在疫苗接种前及接种第二、第三剂后,使用多重血清学检测和假病毒中和试验检测血清中HPV特异性结合抗体与中和抗体。在支气管镜随访期间,对支气管肺泡灌洗液及可用的活检标本进行HPV DNA检测。其中1名患者的血浆样本还通过数字聚合酶链反应检测循环HPV DNA。结果:两名患儿在基线时HPV特异性抗体水平低或检测不到。九价人乳头瘤病毒疫苗诱导了针对疫苗所含HPV型别的强效结合抗体与中和抗体应答。在完成疫苗接种后12至29个月的随访期间,支气管肺泡灌洗液样本中HPV DNA变为不可检测,且在最后一次疫苗接种后干预后未观察到新的乳头状瘤生长。在接受检测的患者中,循环HPV DNA在任何时间点均未被检测到。结论:九价人乳头瘤病毒疫苗在JoRRP患儿中诱导了强效中和抗体应答,并与临床稳定存在时间关联。需开展更大规模研究以明确其临床获益及生物标志物应用价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
幼年发病复发性呼吸道乳头状瘤病(JoRRP)是一种由人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)引起的罕见气道疾病,最常见的病原体为HPV6或HPV11。受影响的儿童通常需要反复进行手术治疗,且目前尚无治愈性疗法。
设计与对象
我们纵向评估了 2 名在 2020 年至 2025 年间接受标签外非价 HPV 疫苗接种 的 JoRRP 儿童。在支气管镜随访期间,对支气管肺泡灌洗液和可用活检样本进行了 HPV DNA 分析。
核心方法
在疫苗接种前及接种第二、第三剂后,使用多重血清学检测和假病毒中和试验检测血清中HPV特异性结合抗体与中和抗体。其中1名患者的血浆样本还通过数字聚合酶链反应检测循环HPV DNA。
主要结果
两个孩子的 HPV 特异性抗体基线水平较低或无法检测到。疫苗接种 诱导针对 疫苗 包含的 HPV 类型的强烈结合和中和抗体反应。在完成 疫苗接种 系列后 12-29 个月的 疫苗接种 随访期间,支气管肺泡灌洗样本中检测不到 HPV DNA,并且在最后一次 疫苗接种 干预后没有观察到新的乳头状瘤生长。在受检患者的任何时间点均未检测到循环 HPV DNA。
解释与创新
非价 HPV 疫苗接种 在 JoRRP 儿童中诱导强烈的中和抗体反应,并暂时与临床稳定相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,非价 HPV 疫苗接种 可能是 JoRRP 的一种有前途的辅助药物 治疗,值得进一步研究以确认其临床益处和生物标志物效用。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其样本量小且缺乏对照组而受到限制。由于该研究的观察性质和缺乏长期随访数据,证据是中等的。

Immunologic Response and Clinical Course After Off-label Nonavalent HPV Vaccination in Juvenile-onset Recurrent Respiratory Papillomatosis Patients.

Authors: Neele M. Thurau, Inga Tometten, Nadine Lübke, Birgitta E. Michels, Daniela Höfler, Fabian Rosing, Peter Sehr, Katharina Remke, Jörg Timm, Steffi Silling
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Juvenile-onset recurrent respiratory papillomatosis (JoRRP) is a rare airway disease caused by human papillomavirus (HPV), most commonly HPV6 or HPV11. Affected children often require repeated surgical procedures, and no curative therapy exists. Off-label HPV vaccination has been proposed as an adjunctive treatment, but vaccine-induced immune responses and the relevance of circulating HPV DNA in benign disease remain unclear. METHODS: We longitudinally evaluated 2 children with JoRRP who received off-label nonavalent HPV vaccination between 2020 and 2025. Serum HPV-specific binding and neutralizing antibodies were measured before vaccination and after the second and third doses using multiplex serology and a pseudovirion-based neutralization assay. During bronchoscopic follow-up, bronchoalveolar lavage fluid and available biopsy samples were analyzed for HPV DNA. In 1 patient, plasma was additionally tested for circulating HPV DNA by digital polymerase chain reaction. RESULTS: Both children had low or undetectable HPV-specific antibodies at baseline. Vaccination induced strong binding and neutralizing antibody responses against vaccine-included HPV types. During post-vaccination follow-up of 12-29 months after completion of the vaccination series, HPV DNA became undetectable in bronchoalveolar lavage samples, and no new papilloma growth was observed after the last post-vaccination intervention. Circulating HPV DNA was not detected at any time point in the patient tested. CONCLUSIONS: Nonavalent HPV vaccination induced robust neutralizing antibody responses in children with JoRRP and was temporally associated with clinical stabilization. Larger studies are needed to clarify clinical benefit and biomarker utility.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Juvenile-onset recurrent respiratory papillomatosis (JoRRP) is a rare airway disease caused by human papillomavirus (HPV), most commonly HPV6 or HPV11. Affected children often require repeated surgical procedures, and no curative therapy exists.
Design and population
We longitudinally evaluated 2 children with JoRRP who received off-label nonavalent HPV vaccination between 2020 and 2025. During bronchoscopic follow-up, bronchoalveolar lavage fluid and available biopsy samples were analyzed for HPV DNA.
Core methods
Serum HPV-specific binding and neutralizing antibodies were measured before vaccination and after the second and third doses using multiplex serology and a pseudovirion-based neutralization assay. In 1 patient, plasma was additionally tested for circulating HPV DNA by digital polymerase chain reaction.
Main results
Both children had low or undetectable HPV-specific antibodies at baseline. Vaccination induced strong binding and neutralizing antibody responses against vaccine-included HPV types. During post-vaccination follow-up of 12-29 months after completion of the vaccination series, HPV DNA became undetectable in bronchoalveolar lavage samples, and no new papilloma growth was observed after the last post-vaccination intervention. Circulating HPV DNA was not detected at any time point in the patient tested.
Interpretation and novelty
Nonavalent HPV vaccination induced robust neutralizing antibody responses in children with JoRRP and was temporally associated with clinical stabilization.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that nonavalent HPV vaccination may be a promising adjunctive treatment for JoRRP, warranting further investigation to confirm its clinical benefit and biomarker utility.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size and lack of a control group. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and the absence of long-term follow-up data.

HPV疫苗对不同年龄、性活跃年限及HIV状态的男男性接触者中常见HPV感染的有效性(2019-2021年)

HPV vaccine effectiveness against prevalent HPV by age, years sexually active before vaccination, and HIV status in gay, bisexual, and other men who have sex with men, 2019-2021
作者： Pranamika Khayargoli, Daniel Grace, Joseph Cox, Alexandra de Pokomandy, Troy Grennan, Trevor A Hart, Rahim Moineddin, David M. Moore, Catharine Chambers, Shelley L. Deeks
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估HPV疫苗对男男性接触者(GBM)人群中常见HPV感染的预防效果,并按年龄、性活跃年限及HIV状态进行分层分析,这些因素可能影响HPV疫苗的效果。
设计与对象
本研究为一项横断面研究,于2019年至2021年间在加拿大蒙特利尔、多伦多和温哥华通过受试者驱动抽样(RDS)招募≥16岁性活跃GBM参与者。
核心方法
参与者自我报告 HPV 疫苗接种 状态并收集自己的肛门 HPV 标本。使用 RDS-II 加权方法估计 患病率,并使用多变量泊松回归计算 患病率 比率 (aPR) 以调整混杂因素。
主要结果
在1,159名年龄为18-83岁的参与者中,17.6%为HIV感染者,且HPV疫苗接种率在不同年龄组存在差异:16-26岁、27-45岁及≥46岁年龄组的接种率分别为62.2%、40.2%和15.0%。在16-26岁GBM中,HPV6/11/16/18感染率在≤23岁时接种及性活跃年限≤5年者中显著降低。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,在更年轻年龄及接近性活跃起始时接种HPV疫苗,可降低GBM人群中可通过HPV疫苗预防的HPV感染率,强调了该人群中早期疫苗接种的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
本研究结果为GBM人群的HPV疫苗接种策略提供科学依据,强调了在更年轻个体及性活跃年限较短者中接种疫苗的潜在效益。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于HPV疫苗接种史的自述数据及RDS抽样方法可能引入的偏倚。尽管如此,由于样本量较大且采用统计调整,证据强度为中等。

HPV vaccine effectiveness against prevalent HPV by age, years sexually active before vaccination, and HIV status in gay, bisexual, and other men who have sex with men, 2019-2021

Authors: Pranamika Khayargoli, Daniel Grace, Joseph Cox, Alexandra de Pokomandy, Troy Grennan, Trevor A Hart, Rahim Moineddin, David M. Moore, Catharine Chambers, Shelley L. Deeks
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background We compared the prevalence of vaccine-preventable anal human papillomavirus (HPV) types among gay, bisexual, and other men who have sex with men (GBM) living in large cities in Canada from 2019 to 2021 by age and HIV status. Methods Sexually-active GBM ≥16 years were recruited in Montreal, Toronto, and Vancouver using respondent-driven sampling (RDS). Participants self-reported HPV vaccination and self-collected anal HPV specimens. We estimated the prevalence of vaccine-preventable types for participants during the 2019 to 2021 period. We report RDS-II-weighted prevalence and confounder-adjusted prevalence ratios (aPRs) comparing vaccinated (≥1 dose) and unvaccinated GBM using multivariable Poisson regression. Results Among 1,159 participants aged 18-83 (median 35 years), 17.6% were living with HIV, and 62.2%, 40.2% and 15.0% were vaccinated in the 16-26, 27-45, and ≥46-year groups, respectively. Among GBM 16-26 years, HPV6/11/16/18 prevalence was lower among those vaccinated ≤23 years (aPR= 0.40, 95% CI: 0.20-0.77), ≥3 years since first dose (aPR=0.39; 95% CI: 0.18-0.85), and those with ≤5 years of sexual activity before vaccination (aPR=0.47; 95%CI: 0.25-0.89). HPV6/11/16/18/31/33/45/52/58 prevalence was lower among those vaccinated ≤26 years in GBM aged 27-45 years (aPR= 0.81, 95% CI, 0.61-1.08), though this did not reach statistical significance. Conclusions Five years after implementation of Canadian HPV vaccination programs for younger GBM, anal HPV prevalence was lower among those vaccinated at younger ages and near sexual debut.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the effectiveness of HPV vaccines against prevalent HPV types in gay, bisexual, and other men who have sex with men (GBM) by age, years sexually active before vaccination, and HIV status, as these factors may influence vaccine impact.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among sexually-active GBM aged ≥16 years in Montreal, Toronto, and Vancouver from 2019 to 2021, using respondent-driven sampling (RDS) to recruit participants.
Core methods
Participants self-reported HPV vaccination status and collected their own anal HPV specimens. Prevalence was estimated using RDS-II-weighted methods, and prevalence ratios (aPRs) were calculated with multivariable Poisson regression to adjust for confounders.
Main results
Among 1,159 participants aged 18-83, 17.6% were living with HIV, and vaccination rates varied by age group: 62.2%, 40.2%, and 15.0% for the 16-26, 27-45, and ≥46-year groups, respectively. Lower prevalence of HPV6/11/16/18 was observed among vaccinated individuals in the 16-26-year group who were vaccinated ≤23 years ago and those with ≤5 years of sexual activity before vaccination.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that HPV vaccination at younger ages and near sexual debut may reduce the prevalence of vaccine-preventable HPV types in GBM, highlighting the importance of early vaccination in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results inform public health strategies for HPV vaccination in GBM, emphasizing the potential benefits of vaccinating younger individuals and those with shorter sexual activity histories.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by self-reported vaccination data and reliance on RDS, which may introduce bias. Despite these limitations, the evidence is moderate in strength due to the large sample size and statistical adjustments.

CTLA-4单倍不足患者中多灶性HPV16阳性鳞状细胞癌:扩展病毒相关性恶性肿瘤谱系

Multifocal HPV16-positive squamous cell carcinomas in CTLA-4 haploinsufficiency: expanding the spectrum of virus-associated malignancies
作者： Christian Arvei Moen, Kristin Greve-Isdahl Mohn, Alexander Marks, Thea E. Hetland Falkenthal, Patrick Juliebø-Jones, Hans Jørgen Aarstad, Svein Erik Moe, Christian Beisland, Daniela Elena Costea, Elisabet Ognedal
摘要中文翻译摘要 细胞毒性T淋巴细胞相关蛋白4(CTLA-4)单倍不足是一种先天性免疫缺陷,以免疫调节失衡、自身免疫、淋巴细胞增殖及恶性肿瘤风险增加为特征。尽管已提出对致瘤性病毒感染的易感性,但CTLA-4单倍不足与人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)驱动的致癌作用之间的关系仍鲜有特征描述。本文报道一例CTLA-4单倍不足患者出现多发HPV相关性恶性肿瘤。回顾了临床、病理、病毒学及遗传学数据。采用全外显子组测序进行生殖系遗传学检测,并对可用的肿瘤标本进行HPV DNA分析。一名50岁时已出现五种不同恶性肿瘤的男性(既往有全秃及自身免疫性甲状腺炎病史),包括EB病毒(EBV)相关性胃霍奇金淋巴瘤、皮肤鳞状细胞癌(SCC)及三处独立的HPV16阳性SCC,累及口腔、肛门及阴茎。生殖系检测确认CTLA-4单倍不足,由一罕见致病CTLA4变异c.406C > T(p.Pro136Ser)所致。未检测到其他与遗传性癌症易感相关的生殖系变异。HPV DNA分析显示三处黏膜SCC标本均为HPV16阳性。本病例扩展了与CTLA-4单倍不足相关的恶性肿瘤谱系,并提示持续性HPV感染可能是该病的未被充分认识的并发症。三处独立HPV16阳性SCC的发生提示CTLA-4缺陷可能使个体易患HPV驱动性致癌,并支持在受累个体中进一步调查HPV感染与癌症预防策略。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述探讨CTLA-4单倍不足与多发HPV相关性恶性肿瘤发生之间的关联,重点关注人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)型别(尤其是HPV16)在致癌中的作用。核心问题是CTLA-4缺陷是否使个体易患HPV驱动性癌症,以及此关联可能被低估的程度。
证据基础与方法
本综述基于一例CTLA-4单倍不足患者出现多发HPV相关性恶性肿瘤的病例报告。回顾了临床、病理、病毒学及遗传学数据,并通过全外显子组测序进行生殖系遗传学检测,对肿瘤标本进行HPV DNA分析。本综述未涉及多项研究数据的荟萃分析或汇总。
共识与结论
该病例报告描述了一名CTLA-4单倍不足患者在不同解剖部位(口腔、肛门及阴茎)出现三处独立HPV16阳性鳞状细胞癌(SCC),同时合并其他HPV相关性恶性肿瘤。HPV DNA分析确认三处黏膜SCC标本均为HPV16阳性,且未检测到其他与遗传性癌症易感相关的生殖系变异。本病例扩展了与CTLA-4缺陷相关的恶性肿瘤谱系,并提示持续性HPV感染可能是该病的未被充分认识的并发症。
争议与缺口
本综述指出,CTLA-4单倍不足与HPV驱动性致癌之间的关联尚缺乏全面认知。尽管该病例报告为单一患者中出现多发HPV相关性恶性肿瘤提供了证据,但未建立因果关联,亦未提供群体水平数据。本研究仅基于单一病例,可能不具普遍代表性,且未提及其他潜在风险因素或机制。
科研与实践启示
CTLA-4单倍不足患者出现三处独立HPV16阳性SCC的现象提示该病可能使个体易患HPV驱动性致癌。这表明需进一步研究HPV感染与CTLA-4缺陷患者癌症预防策略。此外,有必要提高对该类患者持续性HPV感染风险的认识。

Multifocal HPV16-positive squamous cell carcinomas in CTLA-4 haploinsufficiency: expanding the spectrum of virus-associated malignancies

Authors: Christian Arvei Moen, Kristin Greve-Isdahl Mohn, Alexander Marks, Thea E. Hetland Falkenthal, Patrick Juliebø-Jones, Hans Jørgen Aarstad, Svein Erik Moe, Christian Beisland, Daniela Elena Costea, Elisabet Ognedal
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Cytotoxic T-lymphocyte associated protein 4 (CTLA-4) haploinsufficiency is an inborn error of immunity characterized by immune dysregulation, autoimmunity, lymphoproliferation, and an increased risk of malignancy. While susceptibility to oncogenic viral infections has been proposed, the relationship between CTLA-4 haploinsufficiency and human papillomavirus (HPV)-driven carcinogenesis remains poorly characterized. We describe a patient with CTLA-4 haploinsufficiency who developed multiple HPV-associated malignancies. Clinical, pathological, virological, and genetic data were reviewed. Germline genetic testing was performed using whole-exome sequencing, and HPV DNA analysis was conducted on available tumor specimens. A man with a history of alopecia totalis and autoimmune thyroiditis developed five distinct malignancies by the age of 50 years, including Epstein–Barr virus (EBV)-associated gastric Hodgkin lymphoma, cutaneous squamous cell carcinoma (SCC), and three independent HPV16-positive SCCs involving the oral cavity, anus, and penis. Germline testing identified CTLA-4 haploinsufficiency caused by a likely pathogenic CTLA4 variant, c.406C > T (p.Pro136Ser). No additional germline variants associated with hereditary cancer predisposition were detected. HPV DNA analysis demonstrated HPV16 positivity in all three mucosal SCCs. This case expands the spectrum of malignancies associated with CTLA-4 haploinsufficiency and suggests that persistent high-risk HPV infection may represent an underrecognized complication of this disorder. The occurrence of three independent HPV16-positive SCCs raises the possibility that CTLA-4 deficiency predisposes to HPV-driven carcinogenesis and supports further investigation of HPV surveillance and cancer prevention strategies in affected individuals.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review investigates the association between CTLA-4 haploinsufficiency and the development of multiple HPV-associated malignancies, focusing on the role of high-risk HPV types, particularly HPV16, in carcinogenesis. The central question is whether CTLA-4 deficiency predisposes to HPV-driven cancers and how this relationship might be underrecognized.
Evidence base and method
The review is based on a single case report of a patient with CTLA-4 haploinsufficiency who developed multiple HPV-associated malignancies. Clinical, pathological, virological, and genetic data were reviewed, with germline genetic testing conducted via whole-exome sequencing and HPV DNA analysis performed on tumor specimens. The review does not involve a meta-analysis or pooling of data from multiple studies.
Consensus and conclusions
The case report describes a patient with CTLA-4 haploinsufficiency who developed three independent HPV16-positive squamous cell carcinomas (SCCs) in different anatomical sites (oral cavity, anus, and penis), along with other HPV-associated malignancies. HPV DNA analysis confirmed HPV16 positivity in all three mucosal SCCs, and no additional germline variants associated with hereditary cancer predisposition were detected. This case expands the spectrum of malignancies linked to CTLA-4 deficiency and suggests that persistent high-risk HPV infection may be an underrecognized complication of this condition.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights the lack of comprehensive understanding regarding the relationship between CTLA-4 haploinsufficiency and HPV-driven carcinogenesis. While the case report provides evidence of multiple HPV-associated malignancies in a single patient, it does not establish a causal link or provide broader population-level data. The study is limited to a single case, which may not be representative of the general population, and there is no mention of other potential risk factors or mechanisms.
Research and practice implications
The occurrence of three independent HPV16-positive SCCs in a patient with CTLA-4 haploinsufficiency raises the possibility that this condition may predispose individuals to HPV-driven carcinogenesis. This suggests the need for further research into HPV surveillance and cancer prevention strategies in individuals with CTLA-4 deficiency. Additionally, there is a call for increased awareness of the potential for persistent high-risk HPV infection in such patients.

泌尿科 人乳头瘤病毒 相关疾病:诊断、治疗、预防和医疗保健提供者的临床注意事项

Human Papillomavirus-Related Diseases in Urology: Diagnosis, Treatment, Prevention, and Clinical Considerations for Healthcare Providers
作者： Kyu Won Lee, Amit Mani Upadhyay, Sangrak Bae
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 长期以来被认为是一种妇科病原体,但其在男性中的疾病负担却很大且迅速增加。在韩国,过去十年中男性肛门生殖器疣的 发病率 增加了两倍,阴茎癌持续上升,男性 HPV 阳性口咽癌 (OPC) 发病率 已经超过了可比高收入国家的 发病率。这篇综述将 HPV 重新定义为一个紧迫的泌尿科、职业和公共卫生问题。主要借鉴 2023 年韩国性传播感染 (STI) 指南和 2021 年美国疾病控制与预防中心 STI 治疗 指南,并辅以从 PubMed 检索到的原始原始文献,我们解决了:(1) 男性特异性流行病学转变;(2)非性和职业性的传播途径,特别强调手术烟雾;(3)泌尿外科实践中循证感染控制;(4)肛门生殖器疣的临床治疗;(5)男性HPV 疫苗接种政策、国际比较及成本效益;(6) 未来的研究重点,包括 OPC 预防、HPV 和男性不育,以及泌尿科特定感染控制指南的需求。从 2026 年起,韩国将把 12 岁男孩纳入国家免疫计划,这是韩国首个性别中立的 HPV 疫苗接种 政策。这篇综述强调了泌尿科医师在 HPV 管理中不断扩大且不可或缺的作用,并强调感染控制警惕和性别中立的 疫苗接种 策略对于减少 HPV 相关疾病日益增加的社会负担至关重要。本综述为泌尿科医生提供了一个实用的框架,将 HPV 预防、感染控制和 疫苗接种 咨询纳入常规临床实践。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述将人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)重新定位为紧急的泌尿学、职业和公共卫生关切。内容涵盖男性特异性流行病学转变、非性传播和职业性传播传播途径、泌尿学实践中的循证感染控制、肛生殖器疣的临床管理、男性HPV 疫苗接种政策、国际比较与成本效益分析,以及未来研究优先事项。
证据基础与方法
本综述主要参考2023年韩国《性传播感染(STI)指南》与2021年美国疾病控制与预防中心《STI 治疗指南》,并补充检索自PubMed的原始一手文献,以涵盖上述主题。
共识与结论
综述强调泌尿科医师在HPV管理中日益扩展且不可或缺的作用,并指出感染控制警惕性与性别中立的疫苗接种策略是减轻HPV相关疾病日益增长的社会负担的关键。
争议与缺口
综述指出需制定泌尿学专用感染控制指南,并明确未来研究优先事项,包括口咽癌预防、HPV与男性不育的关联,以及泌尿学专用感染控制指南的制定需求。
科研与实践启示
自2026年起,韩国将在国家免疫规划中纳入12岁男孩——这是韩国首个性别中立的HPV 疫苗接种政策。本综述为泌尿科医师提供实用框架,以将HPV预防、感染控制与疫苗接种咨询纳入日常临床实践。

Human Papillomavirus-Related Diseases in Urology: Diagnosis, Treatment, Prevention, and Clinical Considerations for Healthcare Providers

Authors: Kyu Won Lee, Amit Mani Upadhyay, Sangrak Bae
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) has long been regarded as a gynecologic pathogen, yet its disease burden in men is substantial and rapidly increasing. In Korea, the prevalence of anogenital warts in men has tripled over the past decade, penile cancer continues to rise, and HPV-positive oropharyngeal cancer (OPC) incidence in men has already exceeded that of cervical cancer in comparable high-income countries. This review reframes HPV as an urgent urological, occupational, and public health concern. Drawing primarily on the 2023 Korean Sexually Transmitted Infection (STI) Guidelines and the 2021 U.S. Centers for Disease Control and Prevention STI Treatment Guidelines, supplemented by original primary literature retrieved from PubMed, we address: (1) the male-specific epidemiological shift; (2) nonsexual and occupational transmission routes, with particular emphasis on surgical smoke; (3) evidence-based infection control in urology practice; (4) clinical management of anogenital warts; (5) male HPV vaccination policy, international comparisons, and cost-effectiveness; and (6) future research priorities including OPC prevention, HPV and male infertility, and the need for urology-specific infection control guidelines. From 2026, Korea will include 12-year-old boys in its National Immunization Program—the first gender-neutral HPV vaccination policy in Korea. This review highlights the expanding and indispensable role of urologists in HPV management and emphasizes that infection control vigilance and gender-neutral vaccination strategies are essential to reduce the mounting societal burden of HPV-related diseases. This review provides a practical framework for urologists to integrate HPV prevention, infection control, and vaccination counseling into routine clinical practice.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review reframes HPV as an urgent urological, occupational, and public health concern. It addresses the male-specific epidemiological shift, nonsexual and occupational transmission routes, evidence-based infection control in urology practice, clinical management of anogenital warts, male HPV vaccination policy, international comparisons, and cost-effectiveness, as well as future research priorities.
Evidence base and method
Drawing primarily on the 2023 Korean Sexually Transmitted Infection (STI) Guidelines and the 2021 U.S. Centers for Disease Control and Prevention STI Treatment Guidelines, supplemented by original primary literature retrieved from PubMed, we address the aforementioned topics.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights the expanding and indispensable role of urologists in HPV management and emphasizes that infection control vigilance and gender-neutral vaccination strategies are essential to reduce the mounting societal burden of HPV-related diseases.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies the need for urology-specific infection control guidelines and future research priorities including OPC prevention, HPV and male infertility, and the need for urology-specific infection control guidelines.
Research and practice implications
From 2026, Korea will include 12-year-old boys in its National Immunization Program—the first gender-neutral HPV vaccination policy in Korea. This review provides a practical framework for urologists to integrate HPV prevention, infection control, and vaccination counseling into routine clinical practice.

低收入和中等收入国家的妇科癌症:弥合生存差距

Gynecologic cancers in low- and middle-income countries: Bridging the survival gap.
作者： Dewi Setiawati, Wachyudi Muchsin, Alfi Sophan, Alfi Sophian
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
妇科恶性肿瘤——包括宫颈癌、子宫内膜癌、卵巢癌、阴道癌和外阴癌——在低收入和中等收入国家(LMICs)中是一个主要且大部分可预防的死亡来源。
证据基础与方法
本叙事综述整合了PubMed/MEDLINE、Embase、Cochrane图书馆和世界卫生组织(WHO)数据库的证据,优先收录2015年1月至2024年12月发表的文献,重点关注疾病负担、卫生系统约束和政策相关干预措施。
共识与结论
LMICs account for approximately 94% of global cervical cancer deaths, with the highest mortality observed in East Africa (age-standardized mortality rate 35.3 per 100,000).
争议与缺口
持续的生存差异由晚期诊断、有限的筛查覆盖、放疗基础设施不足、人力资源短缺以及获取基本和新型疗法受限所驱动。
科研与实践启示
减少这些不平等需要政策驱动的系统层面改革,优先预防、加强服务提供、扩大公平获得医疗保健的机会,并将国家战略与全球框架(如WHO的90-70-90目标)对齐,以实现LMICs可持续的癌症控制。

Gynecologic cancers in low- and middle-income countries: Bridging the survival gap.

Authors: Dewi Setiawati, Wachyudi Muchsin, Alfi Sophan, Alfi Sophian
Original abstract (English)
Gynecologic malignancies-including cervical, endometrial, ovarian, vaginal, and vulvar cancers-represent a major and largely preventable source of mortality in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs). In 2022, an estimated 1.47 million new cases and over 680,000 deaths were reported globally, with mortality disproportionately concentrated in resource-constrained settings. The survival gap between LMICs and high-income countries (HICs) remains profound, reflecting systemic policy and health system failures in prevention, early detection, and access to treatment. This narrative review synthesizes evidence from PubMed/MEDLINE, Embase, Cochrane Library, and World Health Organization (WHO) databases, prioritizing literature published between January 2015 and December 2024, with a focus on epidemiology, health system constraints, and policy-relevant interventions. LMICs account for approximately 94% of global cervical cancer deaths, with the highest mortality observed in East Africa (age-standardized mortality rate 35.3 per 100,000). Persistent survival disparities are driven by late-stage diagnosis, limited screening coverage, inadequate radiotherapy infrastructure, workforce shortages, and restricted access to essential and novel therapies. However, evidence demonstrates that cost-effective and scalable interventions-including single-dose human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination, screen-and-treat strategies, HPV self-sampling, task-shifting models, and international training partnerships-can substantially improve outcomes when embedded within national cancer control policies and supported by sustainable financing mechanisms. Reducing these inequities requires policy-driven, system-level reforms that prioritize prevention, strengthen service delivery, and expand equitable access to care, aligning national strategies with global frameworks such as the WHO 90-70-90 targets to achieve sustainable cancer control in LMICs.

HPV循环肿瘤DNA作为头颈部鳞状细胞癌潜在预后与预测生物标志物:系统性综述

HPV circulating tumor DNA as a potential prognostic and predictive biomarker in head and neck squamous cell carcinoma: a systematic review.
作者： Daria Maria Filippini, Francesca Carosi, Miriam Tomaciello, Andrea Monte, Matteo Fermi, Edoardo Serafini, Francesco Chiari, Giulia Querzoli, Achille Tarsitano, Giuseppe Mercante
摘要中文翻译目的:HPV循环肿瘤DNA(HPVctDNA)已成为HPV相关头颈部鳞状细胞癌(HNSCC)中具有前景的预后生物标志物。本系统性综述旨在综合HPVctDNA在HNSCC管理中的诊断准确性与预后价值的现有证据。材料/方法:我们系统性回顾了2012年1月至2025年9月期间发表于PubMed数据库的研究。符合条件的研究被评估其设计、原发肿瘤部位与分期、治疗模式、HPVctDNA检测方法、诊断准确性(敏感性与特异性)及报告的临床终点。进行描述性综合分析;敏感性与特异性标准化为比例并按组别汇总为中位值。结果:共纳入60项研究,涉及8,234名患者,其中41项(68.3%)仅针对口咽鳞状细胞癌(OPSCC),17项(28.3%)涵盖混合HPV相关HNSCC亚部位及HPV阳性原发灶不明癌。纳入研究的中位随访时间为23个月。在纳入研究中,19项为回顾性研究(31.7%),33项为前瞻性研究(55.0%),另有少量横断面研究与随机对照临床试验。总体而言,40项研究(66.7%)评估了HPVctDNA在根治性治疗情境中的作用。最常见的样本类型为血浆,共55项研究(91.7%)采用,另有5项研究纳入唾液。检测方法多样:40项采用数字液滴PCR(ddPCR),16项使用定量PCR(qPCR),4项应用NGS相关检测。大多数研究(38项,63.3%)评估了HPVctDNA的预后价值,仅4项(6.7%)在筛查或诊断情境中评估HPVctDNA。在可评估研究中,中位敏感性为91.1%,中位特异性为99.4%。在仅限OPSCC的研究中,中位敏感性与特异性分别为89.4%与99.4%。HPVctDNA水平在治疗期间或治疗后的动态变化与结局一致相关:清除或持续阴性与更高的治疗反应率、改善的无进展生存期及总生存期相关,而持续阳性或水平升高则预测疾病进展与复发。结论:HPVctDNA在HPV相关HNSCC(尤其是OPSCC)中展现出高诊断与预后准确性,支持其用于预后评估、治疗监测及早期复发检测。然而,仍需前瞻性干预性研究证明HPVctDNA指导的治疗决策能改善临床结局,方可常规实施。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
HPV循环肿瘤DNA(HPVctDNA)已成为HPV相关头颈部鳞状细胞癌(HNSCC)中具有前景的预后生物标志物。本系统性综述旨在综合HPVctDNA在HNSCC管理中的诊断准确性与预后价值的现有证据。
证据基础与方法
材料/方法:我们系统性回顾了2012年1月至2025年9月期间发表于PubMed数据库的研究。符合条件的研究被评估其设计、原发肿瘤部位与分期、治疗模式、HPVctDNA检测方法、诊断准确性(敏感性与特异性)及报告的临床终点。检测方法多样:40项采用数字液滴PCR(ddPCR),16项使用定量PCR(qPCR),4项应用NGS相关检测。
共识与结论
总体而言,40项研究(66.7%)评估了HPVctDNA在根治性治疗情境中的作用。大多数研究(38项,63.3%)评估了HPVctDNA的预后价值,仅4项(6.7%)在筛查或诊断情境中评估HPVctDNA。在可评估研究中,中位敏感性为91.1%,中位特异性为99.4%。在仅限OPSCC的研究中,中位敏感性与特异性分别为89.4%与99.4%。HPVctDNA在HPV相关HNSCC(尤其是OPSCC)中展现出高诊断与预后准确性,支持其用于预后评估、治疗监测及早期复发检测。
争议与缺口
总共分析了 60 项研究,包括 8,234 名患者,其中 41 项 (68.3%) 专门针对口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC),17 项 (28.3%) 包括混合 HPV 相关 HNSCC 亚位点和原发灶不明的 HPV 阳性癌症。纳入研究的中位随访时间为 23 个月。纳入的研究中,回顾性研究19项(31.7%),前瞻性研究33项(55.0%),还有一小部分是横断面和随机临床试验。血浆是最常见的样本类型,有 55 项研究 (91.7%) 进行了分析,而 5 项研究还包括唾液。
科研与实践启示
治疗 期间或之后 HPVctDNA 水平的动态变化始终与结果相关:清除或持续阴性与较高的缓解率、改善的无进展生存期和总生存期相关,而持续阳性或水平升高则预测疾病进展和复发。

HPV circulating tumor DNA as a potential prognostic and predictive biomarker in head and neck squamous cell carcinoma: a systematic review.

Authors: Daria Maria Filippini, Francesca Carosi, Miriam Tomaciello, Andrea Monte, Matteo Fermi, Edoardo Serafini, Francesco Chiari, Giulia Querzoli, Achille Tarsitano, Giuseppe Mercante
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: Human papillomavirus circulating tumor DNA (HPVctDNA) has emerged as a promising prognostic biomarker in HPV-related head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC). This systematic review aimed to synthesize current evidence on the diagnostic accuracy and prognostic value of HPVctDNA in HNSCC management. MATERIAL/METHODS: We systematically reviewed a PubMed-indexed database of studies published between January 2012 and September 2025. Eligible studies were assessed for design, primary tumor site and stage, treatment modality, HPVctDNA detection method, diagnostic accuracy (sensitivity and specificity), and reported clinical endpoints. Descriptive syntheses were performed; sensitivity and specificity were standardized to proportions and summarized as median values per group. RESULTS: A total of 60 studies, including 8,234 patients were analyzed, of which 41 (68.3%) focused exclusively on oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) and 17 (28.3%) included mixed HPV-related HNSCC subsites and HPV-positive cancers of unknown primary. The median follow-up across the included studies was 23 months. Among the included studies, 19 were retrospective (31.7%) and 33 were prospective (55.0%), with a small proportion of cross-sectional and randomized clinical trials. Overall, 40 (66.7%) evaluated the role of HPVctDNA in a curative setting. Plasma was the most common sample type, analyzed in 55 studies (91.7%), while 5 studies also included saliva. Detection methods varied: 40 employed droplet digital PCR (ddPCR), 16 used quantitative PCR (qPCR) and 4 applied NGS-based assays. Most of these studies (38, 63.3%) evaluated the prognostic utility of HPVctDNA, while only 4 (6.7%) assessed HPVctDNA in a screening or diagnostic setting. Regarding diagnostic accuracy, the median sensitivity across evaluable studies was 91.1%, while the median specificity was 99.4%. In OPSCC-only cohorts, the median sensitivity and specificity were 89.4% and 99.4%, respectively. Dynamic changes in HPVctDNA levels during or after treatment were consistently associated with outcomes: clearance or sustained negativity correlated with higher response rates, improved progression-free survival and overall survival, while persistent positivity or increasing levels predicted disease progression and recurrence. CONCLUSIONS: HPVctDNA demonstrates high diagnostic and prognostic accuracy in HPV-related HNSCC, especially OPSCC, supporting its use for prognosis, treatment monitoring and early detection of recurrence. However, prospective interventional studies are still required to demonstrate that HPVctDNA-guided treatment decisions improve clinical outcomes before routine implementation.

This systematic review aimed to synthesize current evidence on the diagnostic accuracy and prognostic value of HPVctDNA in HNSCC management.
Evidence base and method

Eligible studies were assessed for design, primary tumor site and stage, treatment modality, HPVctDNA detection method, diagnostic accuracy (sensitivity and specificity), and reported clinical endpoints. Detection methods varied: 40 employed droplet digital PCR (ddPCR), 16 used quantitative PCR (qPCR) and 4 applied NGS-based assays.
Consensus and conclusions
Overall, 40 (66.7%) evaluated the role of HPVctDNA in a curative setting. Most of these studies (38, 63.3%) evaluated the prognostic utility of HPVctDNA, while only 4 (6.7%) assessed HPVctDNA in a screening or diagnostic setting. Regarding diagnostic accuracy, the median sensitivity across evaluable studies was 91.1%, while the median specificity was 99.4%. In OPSCC-only cohorts, the median sensitivity and specificity were 89.4% and 99.4%, respectively. HPVctDNA demonstrates high diagnostic and prognostic accuracy in HPV-related HNSCC, especially OPSCC, supporting its use for prognosis, treatment monitoring and early detection of recurrence.
Controversies and gaps
A total of 60 studies, including 8,234 patients were analyzed, of which 41 (68.3%) focused exclusively on oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) and 17 (28.3%) included mixed HPV-related HNSCC subsites and HPV-positive cancers of unknown primary. The median follow-up across the included studies was 23 months. Among the included studies, 19 were retrospective (31.7%) and 33 were prospective (55.0%), with a small proportion of cross-sectional and randomized clinical trials. Plasma was the most common sample type, analyzed in 55 studies (91.7%), while 5 studies also included saliva.
Research and practice implications
Dynamic changes in HPVctDNA levels during or after treatment were consistently associated with outcomes: clearance or sustained negativity correlated with higher response rates, improved progression-free survival and overall survival, while persistent positivity or increasing levels predicted disease progression and recurrence.

实体器官移植受者中人乳头瘤病毒相关性肛门发育异常:一种黏膜免疫功能障碍的移植特异性模型

HPV-associated Anal Dysplasia in Solid Organ Transplant Recipients: A Transplant-specific Model of Mucosal Immune Dysfunction
作者： Edward R. Cachay, Nitin Mishra, Holenarasipur R. Vikram, Sandhya R. Nagarakanti, Sami Alasfar, Christina Wu, Karen S. Anderson
摘要中文翻译摘要:由于慢性免疫抑制和病毒控制受损,实体器官移植受者患 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关肛门癌的风险增加。该人群肛门发育不良的自然史和基础生物学仍不清楚,目前的治疗很大程度上是从人类免疫缺陷病毒相关疾病中推断出来的。移植相关的免疫功能障碍与其他免疫功能低下状态有根本不同,它直接影响对 HPV 控制至关重要的 T 细胞激活、抗原呈递和免疫记忆。在本概述中,我们整合了 HPV 驱动的致癌作用、HIV 相关肛门疾病和移植免疫学的见解,提出了粘膜免疫功能障碍的移植特异性框架。在该模型中,HPV 免疫逃避协同慢性免疫抑制,损害 抗病毒药物 细胞免疫,促进持续感染,以及以 T 细胞功能障碍、抗原呈递受损、调节性免疫极化和代谢重编程为特征的促肿瘤微环境。假设这些过程促进高级鳞状上皮内病变的进展,这是肛门癌的直接前兆。该框架为解释临床观察到的实体器官移植受者肛门发育不良的持续性和复发提供了生物学基础,强调了关键的知识差距,并可能为这一高危人群的移植特异性筛查、治疗策略和前瞻性研究提供信息。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
由于慢性免疫抑制和病毒控制受损,实体器官移植受者患 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关肛门癌的风险增加。
证据基础与方法
该人群中肛门发育异常的自然史及其潜在生物学机制仍不明确,当前的管理策略主要基于从人类免疫缺陷病毒相关疾病中外推而来。
共识与结论
移植相关的免疫功能障碍与其他免疫功能低下状态有根本不同,它直接影响对 HPV 控制至关重要的 T 细胞激活、抗原呈递和免疫记忆。
争议与缺口
在本概述中,我们整合了 HPV 驱动的致癌作用、HIV 相关肛门疾病和移植免疫学的见解,提出了粘膜免疫功能障碍的移植特异性框架。
科研与实践启示
假设这些过程促进高级鳞状上皮内病变的进展,这是肛门癌的直接前兆。

HPV-associated Anal Dysplasia in Solid Organ Transplant Recipients: A Transplant-specific Model of Mucosal Immune Dysfunction

Authors: Edward R. Cachay, Nitin Mishra, Holenarasipur R. Vikram, Sandhya R. Nagarakanti, Sami Alasfar, Christina Wu, Karen S. Anderson
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT: Solid organ transplant recipients face an increased risk of human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated anal cancer because of chronic immunosuppression and impaired viral control. The natural history and underlying biology of anal dysplasia in this population remain poorly defined, and current management is largely extrapolated from human immunodeficiency virus-associated disease. Transplant-related immune dysfunction differs fundamentally from other immunocompromised states, with direct effects on T-cell activation, antigen presentation, and immune memory that are central to HPV control. In this overview, we integrate insights from HPV-driven carcinogenesis, HIV-associated anal disease, and transplant immunology to propose a transplant-specific framework of mucosal immune dysfunction. In this model, HPV immune evasion synergizes chronic immunosuppression to impair antiviral cellular immunity, promoting persistent infection, and a protumorigenic microenvironment characterized by T-cell dysfunction, impaired antigen presentation, regulatory immune polarization, and metabolic reprogramming. These processes are hypothesized to facilitate progression to high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions, the immediate precursor to anal cancer. This framework provides a biologic basis to interpret clinically observed persistence and recurrence of anal dysplasia in solid organ transplant recipients, highlights key knowledge gaps and may inform transplant-specific screening, therapeutic strategies, and prospective studies in this high-risk population.

Solid organ transplant recipients face an increased risk of human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated anal cancer due to chronic immunosuppression and impaired viral control.
Evidence base and method

Consensus and conclusions

Controversies and gaps

Research and practice implications
These processes are hypothesized to facilitate progression to high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions, the immediate precursor to anal cancer.

HPV 相关口咽鳞状细胞癌:AJCC 第 9 版 TNM 分期的影像框架

HPV-associated Oropharyngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma: An Imaging Framework for AJCC Version 9 TNM Staging
作者： Pranjal Rai, Adam L. Holtzman, Alok A. Bhatt
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述检查了 HPV 相关口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC),重点关注影像学在 AJCC 第 9 版系统下的 TNM 分期中的作用,特别讨论分期如何影响 治疗 对 HPV 阳性疾病的决策。
证据基础与方法
本文为一篇叙述性综述,概述了AJCC和UICC第9版TNM分期标准在HPV相关OPSCC中的应用,强调了基于影像学(CT、MRI和PET)评估肿瘤范围、淋巴结疾病和淋巴结外扩展(iENE)的重要性。
共识与结论
该综述认同HPV相关OPSCC在生物学特性和预后方面与HPV阴性疾病截然不同,且分期直接影响治疗选择;同时认为AJCC第9版将iENE纳入淋巴结分期,并保留了第八版的T和M类别定义。
争议与缺口
由于读片者间差异和诊断性能不完善,影像学检测到的淋巴结外扩展(iENE)的可重复性及其增量预后价值仍存在不确定性,需要更高级别的证据来验证其作为临床N类别修饰因子的作用。
科研与实践启示
该综述强调需要进一步的高级别证据来确立iENE的预后价值,并呼吁在临床实践中谨慎解读;同时强调多学科协作在选择手术切除与非手术治疗中的重要性。

HPV-associated Oropharyngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma: An Imaging Framework for AJCC Version 9 TNM Staging

Authors: Pranjal Rai, Adam L. Holtzman, Alok A. Bhatt
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) is biologically and prognostically distinct from HPV-negative disease, and staging directly impacts treatment. The American Joint Committee on Cancer (AJCC) and Union for International Cancer Control version 9 TNM staging system incorporates unequivocal imaging-detected and/or clinically detected extranodal extension (iENE) into clinical nodal staging and definitive pathologic extranodal extension into pathologic nodal staging. Additionally, it provides more explicit anatomic definitions for advanced local disease (clinical T4) while maintaining T and M category definitions from the eighth edition. Radiologists play a central role in the multidisciplinary team by evaluating endophytic tumor extent, regional nodal disease and iENE, and distant metastasis using CT, MRI, and PET. This article reviews clinically relevant oropharyngeal anatomy and proposes a practical radiologic approach to assigning T, N, and M categories in HPV-associated OPSCC. It emphasizes cautious interpretation of iENE because of interreader variability and imperfect diagnostic performance, with further high-grade evidence needed to establish its consistently reproducible, incremental prognostic value as a clinical N category modifier. It also highlights multidisciplinary factors that guide selection of surgical resection versus nonsurgical therapy. Posttreatment evaluation is discussed in the context of blood-based biomarkers such as tumor tissue-modified viral HPV DNA. The goal of this article is to provide a practical, staging-focused imaging framework for evaluation of HPV-associated OPSCC. Keywords: CT, MR Imaging, Head/Neck, Neck, Pharynx, Surgery, Human Papillomavirus, Squamous Cell Carcinoma, Oropharynx, Transoral Robotic Surgery, Oropharyngeal Carcinoma, TTMV HPV DNA © RSNA, 2026.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines HPV-associated oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC), focusing on the role of imaging in TNM staging under the AJCC version 9 system, specifically addressing how staging impacts treatment decisions for HPV-positive disease.
Evidence base and method
The article is a narrative review that outlines the AJCC and UICC version 9 TNM staging criteria for HPV-associated OPSCC, emphasizing imaging-based evaluation of tumor extent, nodal disease, and extranodal extension (iENE) using CT, MRI, and PET.
Consensus and conclusions
The review agrees that HPV-associated OPSCC is biologically and prognostically distinct from HPV-negative disease, with staging directly influencing treatment choices; it also concludes that AJCC version 9 incorporates iENE into nodal staging and maintains T and M category definitions from the eighth edition.
Controversies and gaps
There is uncertainty regarding the consistent reproducibility and incremental prognostic value of imaging-detected extranodal extension (iENE) due to interreader variability and imperfect diagnostic performance, necessitating higher-grade evidence to validate its role as a clinical N category modifier.
Research and practice implications
The review highlights the need for further high-grade evidence to establish the prognostic utility of iENE and calls for cautious interpretation in clinical practice; it also emphasizes the importance of multidisciplinary collaboration in selecting between surgical resection and nonsurgical therapy.

mRNA疫苗疫苗在诱导免疫应答和预防致癌结局中的有效性:一项系统综述与荟萃分析

Efficacy of mRNA-based HPV Vaccines in Inducing Immune Response and Preventing Oncogenic Outcomes: A systematic review and meta-analysis
作者： Valentina Yurina, Efta Triastuti, Diana Lyrawati, Suryanata Kesuma, Nashi Widodo, Delvac Oceandy
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述关注人乳头瘤病毒(人乳头瘤病毒,HPV),重点分析高危型(如HPV-16、HPV-18)与宫颈癌、生殖器及口咽部恶性肿瘤等致癌结局的关联,以及HPV感染与生殖器疣在宿主中的发生。核心问题评估mRNA基础HPV疫苗疫苗在临床前动物模型中的免疫原性与保护效力,具体关注其预防致癌感染及诱导已建立肿瘤消退的能力。
证据基础与方法
This systematic review and meta-analysis is registered in PROSPERO (CRD420261362037, 14 July 2026) and follows PRISMA 2020 guidelines. A systematic search was conducted in PubMed, Scopus, Europe PMC, and ScienceDirect. Eligible studies included in vivo preclinical experimental research evaluating mRNA vaccines targeting high-risk HPV types (HPV-16 and HPV-18) in animal models. Immunogenicity (CD8+ T-cell responses and IgG titers) and oncogenic outcome (tumor volume and survival rate) data were extracted. The risk of bias was assessed using SYRCLE’s tool, and a random-effects meta-analysis calculated standardized mean differences (SMD) with heterogeneity evaluated via the I2 statistic.
共识与结论
汇总结果表明,mRNA 疫苗 诱导高免疫原性,显着扩大抗原特异性 CD8+ T 细胞群体并诱导强劲的 IFN γ 分泌。在攻击模型中,肿瘤体积显着减少(SMD -2.305;95% CI:-2.64至-1.97),病毒载量几乎完全减少。通过脂质纳米粒子或脂多聚复合物递送的高危型HPV mRNA 疫苗显示出作为预防剂和治疗剂的有希望的治疗潜力。
争议与缺口
该审查没有报告 免疫应答 的长期安全性数据、耐久性,或与已确定的预防性病毒样颗粒 (VLP) 疫苗 在人类中的疗效比较。此外,证据基础仅限于临床前动物模型,这使得人类临床环境的转化潜力存在不确定性。
科研与实践启示
这些发现支持基于 mRNA 的 HPV 疫苗 进入临床试验,特别是针对已确定的感染和病变的治疗应用。优先研究领域包括评估 免疫应答 的长期安全性、耐久性以及在人群中的比较疗效。监测 和诊断策略应结合免疫原性和肿瘤消退指标,以监测 疫苗 在临床试验中的表现。

Efficacy of mRNA-based HPV Vaccines in Inducing Immune Response and Preventing Oncogenic Outcomes: A systematic review and meta-analysis

Authors: Valentina Yurina, Efta Triastuti, Diana Lyrawati, Suryanata Kesuma, Nashi Widodo, Delvac Oceandy
Original abstract (English)
Background Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection represents a substantial global health burden, being the primary etiological agent of cervical cancer, anogenital and oropharyngeal malignancies. Although prophylactic virus-like particle (VLP) vaccines have been successfully implemented, they lack therapeutic efficacy against established infections and lesions in the host. The messenger RNA (mRNA) vaccine platform has emerged as a promising alternative, offering rapid manufacturing and the ability to induce potent cellular immune responses required for effective therapeutic outcomes. Objective This systematic review aimed to synthesize and evaluate preclinical evidence regarding the immunogenicity and protective efficacy of mRNA-based HPV vaccines in animal models, focusing on their ability to prevent oncogenic infections and induce regression of established tumors. Methods The study is registered in Prospero (CRD420261362037, 14 July 2026). A systematic search of PubMed, Scopus, Europe PMC, and ScienceDirect databases was conducted according to the PRISMA 2020 guidelines. Eligible studies included in vivo preclinical experimental research evaluating mRNA vaccines targeting high-risk HPV types (HPV-16 and HPV-18) in animal models. Immunogenicity (CD8+ T-cell responses and IgG titers) and oncogenic outcome (tumor volume and survival rate) data were extracted. The risk of bias was assessed using the SYRCLE’s risk-of-bias tool. The meta-analysis utilized a random-effects model to calculate standardized mean differences (SMD) and evaluate heterogeneity via the I 2 statistic. Result and Conclusion Ten studies met the inclusion criteria and provided detailed data for the analysis. mRNA vaccines have demonstrated high immunogenicity, significantly expanding antigen-specific CD8+ T-cell populations and inducing robust IFN gamma secretion. The pooled results indicated a significant reduction in tumor volume (SMD -2.305; 95% CI: −2.64 to −1.97) and near-complete viral load reduction in the challenge models. High-risk HPV mRNA vaccines delivered via lipid nanoparticles (LNPs) or lipopolyplexes (LPPs) have a promising therapeutic potential as both prophylactic and therapeutic agents, supporting their advancement to clinical trials.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human papillomaviruses (HPV), focusing on high-risk types (e.g., HPV-16, HPV-18) and their association with oncogenic outcomes such as cervical, anogenital, and oropharyngeal malignancies, as well as HPV infection and genital warts in the host. The central question evaluates the immunogenicity and protective efficacy of mRNA-based HPV vaccines in preclinical animal models, specifically their ability to prevent oncogenic infections and induce regression of established tumors.
Evidence base and method
This systematic review and meta-analysis is registered in PROSPERO (CRD420261362037, 14 July 2026) and follows PRISMA 2020 guidelines. A systematic search was conducted in PubMed, Scopus, Europe PMC, and ScienceDirect. Eligible studies included in vivo preclinical experimental research evaluating mRNA vaccines targeting high-risk HPV types (HPV-16 and HPV-18) in animal models. Immunogenicity (CD8+ T-cell responses and IgG titers) and oncogenic outcome (tumor volume and survival rate) data were extracted. The risk of bias was assessed using SYRCLE’s tool, and a random-effects meta-analysis calculated standardized mean differences (SMD) with heterogeneity evaluated via the I2 statistic.
Consensus and conclusions
The pooled results demonstrate that mRNA vaccines induce high immunogenicity, significantly expanding antigen-specific CD8+ T-cell populations and inducing robust IFN gamma secretion. There is a significant reduction in tumor volume (SMD -2.305; 95% CI: −2.64 to −1.97) and near-complete viral load reduction in challenge models. High-risk HPV mRNA vaccines delivered via lipid nanoparticles or lipopolyplexes show promising therapeutic potential as both prophylactic and therapeutic agents.
Controversies and gaps
The review does not report long-term safety data, durability of immune responses, or comparative efficacy against established prophylactic virus-like particle (VLP) vaccines in humans. Additionally, the evidence base is limited to preclinical animal models, leaving uncertainties about translational potential to human clinical settings.
Research and practice implications
The findings support the advancement of mRNA-based HPV vaccines to clinical trials, particularly for therapeutic applications against established infections and lesions. Priority research areas include evaluating long-term safety, durability of immune responses, and comparative efficacy in human populations. Surveillance and diagnostic strategies should incorporate immunogenicity and tumor regression metrics to monitor vaccine performance in clinical trials.

将非编码 RNA 分析与 HPV 基因分型相结合,用于 宫颈癌 风险分层和早期检测

Integrating non-coding RNA profiling with HPV genotyping for cervical cancer risk stratification and early detection
作者： Priyanshi Singh, Brij Bhushan, Anoop Kumar, Gauri Misra, Neelima Mishra
摘要中文翻译宫颈癌 是全球重大公共卫生问题,也是全球女性癌症相关死亡的主要原因。尽管当前诊断方法已改善 诊断,但仍无法有效区分一过性感染与进展为恶性病变的感染。此局限性凸显出需要更精准的诊断方法,以支持早期干预、风险评估及临床决策制定。HPV基因分型可识别特定高危型病毒株,为感染风险评估、疾病 监测 及 疫苗 评估提供关键信息。然而,HPV基因分型无法指示病毒致癌活性或细胞转化。另一方面,非编码RNA(如miRNA、lncRNA及circRNA)作为HPV介导致癌过程中的关键调控分子,其在生物流体中的稳定性支持其作为无创液体活检诊断的应用。本综述探讨将HPV基因分型与非编码RNA谱分析整合为一种多组学诊断方法,用于 宫颈癌 风险分层。通过将病毒基因型信息与 宿主 分子反应相结合,此整合策略有望提高诊断准确性、增强患者风险分层,并促进更个性化的筛查与管理。尽管单个非编码RNA生物标志物已显示出与HPV相关宫颈致癌过程的有希望关联,但支持其与HPV基因分型联合临床应用的证据仍有限,需进一步前瞻性验证。尽管如此,整合病毒与 宿主 分子生物标志物仍可能成为改善分子风险评估并支持更个性化 宫颈癌 筛查策略开发的有用方法。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
宫颈癌 是全球重大公共卫生问题,也是全球女性癌症相关死亡的主要原因。本综述探讨将HPV基因分型与非编码RNA谱分析整合为一种多组学诊断方法,用于 宫颈癌 风险分层。通过将病毒基因型信息与 宿主 分子反应相结合,此整合策略有望提高诊断准确性、增强患者风险分层,并促进更个性化的筛查与管理。
证据基础与方法
尽管单个 ncRNA 生物标志物已显示出与 HPV 相关宫颈癌发生有良好的关联,但支持其与 HPV 基因分型联合临床应用的证据仍然有限,需要进一步的前瞻性验证。该综述是一篇叙述性综述,将非编码 RNA 分析与 HPV 基因分型相结合,以探索它们在 宫颈癌 风险分层中的综合潜力。
共识与结论
这一限制凸显了对更精确的诊断方法的需求,以支持早期干预、风险评估和明智的临床决策。HPV 基因分型包括识别特定的高风险病毒株,为感染风险评估、疾病 监测 和 疫苗 评估提供重要信息。
争议与缺口
然而,它并不表明病毒致癌活性或细胞转化。病毒和 宿主 分子生物标志物的整合代表了一种潜在有用的方法,可改善分子风险评估并支持开发更个性化的 宫颈癌 筛查策略。
科研与实践启示
尽管目前的诊断方法已经改进了 诊断,但在区分暂时性感染和进展为恶性肿瘤的感染方面受到限制。

Integrating non-coding RNA profiling with HPV genotyping for cervical cancer risk stratification and early detection

Authors: Priyanshi Singh, Brij Bhushan, Anoop Kumar, Gauri Misra, Neelima Mishra
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer is a major global health concern and a leading cause of cancer-related deaths among women worldwide. Although current diagnostic methods have improved diagnosis but are limited in distinguishing transient infections from those progressing toward malignancy. This limitation highlights the need for more precise diagnostic approaches that can support early intervention, risk assessment, and informed clinical decision-making. HPV genotyping includes identification of specific high-risk viral strains, providing essential information for infection risk assessment, disease surveillance, and vaccine evaluation. However, it does not indicate viral oncogenic activity or cellular transformation. On the other hand, ncRNAs such as miRNAs, lncRNAs, and circRNAs serve as key regulatory molecules in HPV-mediated carcinogenesis. Moreover, their stability in biological fluids supports their use as non-invasive, liquid biopsy-based diagnostics. This narrative review explores the potential of integrating HPV genotyping with ncRNA profiling as a multi-omics diagnostic approach for cervical cancer risk stratification. By combining information on viral genotype with host molecular responses, this integrated strategy may improve diagnostic accuracy, enhance patient risk stratification, and facilitate more personalized screening and management. Although individual ncRNA biomarkers have shown promising associations with HPV-associated cervical carcinogenesis, evidence supporting their combined clinical application with HPV genotyping remains limited and requires further prospective validation. Nevertheless, the integration of viral and host molecular biomarkers represents a potentially useful approach for improving molecular risk assessment and supporting the development of more personalized cervical cancer screening strategies.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Cervical cancer is a major global health concern and a leading cause of cancer-related deaths among women worldwide. This narrative review explores the potential of integrating HPV genotyping with ncRNA profiling as a multi-omics diagnostic approach for cervical cancer risk stratification. By combining information on viral genotype with host molecular responses, this integrated strategy may improve diagnostic accuracy, enhance patient risk stratification, and facilitate more personalized screening and management.
Evidence base and method
Although individual ncRNA biomarkers have shown promising associations with HPV-associated cervical carcinogenesis, evidence supporting their combined clinical application with HPV genotyping remains limited and requires further prospective validation. The review is a narrative review that integrates non-coding RNA profiling with HPV genotyping to explore their combined potential in cervical cancer risk stratification.
Consensus and conclusions
This limitation highlights the need for more precise diagnostic approaches that can support early intervention, risk assessment, and informed clinical decision-making. HPV genotyping includes identification of specific high-risk viral strains, providing essential information for infection risk assessment, disease surveillance, and vaccine evaluation.
Controversies and gaps
However, it does not indicate viral oncogenic activity or cellular transformation. The integration of viral and host molecular biomarkers represents a potentially useful approach for improving molecular risk assessment and supporting the development of more personalized cervical cancer screening strategies.
Research and practice implications
Although current diagnostic methods have improved diagnosis but are limited in distinguishing transient infections from those progressing toward malignancy.

沙特阿拉伯成年人的 HPV 知识、焦虑和 疫苗 接受度:一项横断面研究。

HPV Knowledge, Anxiety and Vaccine Acceptance Among Adults in Saudi Arabia: A Cross-Sectional Study.
作者： Abdullah E. Alsobaie, Fahad A. Alqurashi, Muath S. D. Alotaibi, Mohammad I. A. Alwuthaynani, Ahmed R. G. Alotaibi, Ahmed N. Alghamdi, Ali H. S. Alzahrani, Ahmad F. Eweas, Youssef A. S. Abdel-Moneim, Naifa Alenazi
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 会导致各种感染和严重的健康并发症,包括癌症。疫苗的犹豫和焦虑继续阻碍着它的控制。本研究评估了沙特阿拉伯成年人群(包括公民和非沙特居民)的 HPV 相关知识、感染焦虑和 疫苗 犹豫度,以及接触皮肤和生殖器疣的情况。共有 2014 名成年人(≥ 18 岁)完成了一项在线调查,内容涵盖人口统计、HPV 知识以及适用于衡量 HPV 特定问题的改良版广泛性焦虑症 7 (GAD-7) 量表。皮肤疣很常见,特别是在 21-50 岁的女性中,而生殖器疣则较少见,在 21-40 岁年龄组中达到高峰。18-30 岁参与者的焦虑程度最高。关于 HPV 传播 和 疫苗 益处存在显着的知识差距。年轻的参与者表现出更好的理解力,但男性不太了解 疫苗 的可用性。医疗保健专业人员和医学生展示了卓越的知识。多项 Logistic 回归显示,性别是 疫苗 意愿的唯一显着预测因子 (p < 0.001)。男性比女性更有可能说“是”(aOR = 1.409,p = 0.009)和“否”(aOR = 1.633,p < 0.001),这表明女性不成比例地犹豫(“不确定”)。高中教育(相对于医学背景)与较低的意愿相关(aOR = 0.631,p = 0.030)。总体而言,研究结果强调需要开展有针对性的教育活动,以提高 HPV 意识并促进 疫苗 的接受。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述调查了沙特阿拉伯成年人中的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV)、其相关疾病(包括宫颈上皮内瘤变和 HPV 相关癌症)和 疫苗 犹豫度,重点关注知识差距、感染焦虑和 疫苗 接受度的决定因素。
证据基础与方法
该综述综合了沙特阿拉伯 2014 名成年人(≥18 岁)的横断面调查数据,包括人口统计、HPV 知识评估以及通过改良的广泛性焦虑症 7 (GAD-7) 量表测量的 HPV 特异性焦虑。它采用多项逻辑回归来识别 疫苗 意愿的预测因子。
共识与结论
年轻的参与者表现出更好的 HPV 知识,而男性则表现出对 疫苗 可用性的认识较低和更高的犹豫。医疗保健专业人员和医学生拥有丰富的知识。性别是 疫苗 意愿的唯一显着预测因素 (p < 0.001),与女性相比,男性更有可能接受或拒绝 疫苗接种。
争议与缺口
关于人乳头瘤病毒疫苗的获益(尤其是在非医疗背景人群中),仍存在知识缺口。该研究未涉及地区差异、文化障碍或干预后的长期行为结果。焦虑水平在18-30岁年龄组中最高,但其与人乳头瘤病毒疫苗接种犹豫之间的因果关系尚未确立。
科研与实践启示
亟需针对性的宣教活动以提升人乳头瘤病毒疫苗的认知度,重点关注男性及非医疗背景人群。疾控部门应监测人乳头瘤病毒疫苗接种接受度趋势,并纠正关于人乳头瘤病毒疫苗及其有效性的误解。需进一步研究评估焦虑对疫苗接种意愿的影响,并设计文化适配的干预措施。

HPV Knowledge, Anxiety and Vaccine Acceptance Among Adults in Saudi Arabia: A Cross-Sectional Study.

Authors: Abdullah E. Alsobaie, Fahad A. Alqurashi, Muath S. D. Alotaibi, Mohammad I. A. Alwuthaynani, Ahmed R. G. Alotaibi, Ahmed N. Alghamdi, Ali H. S. Alzahrani, Ahmad F. Eweas, Youssef A. S. Abdel-Moneim, Naifa Alenazi
Original abstract (English)
Human Papillomavirus (HPV) causes various infections and serious health complications, including cancer. Vaccine hesitancy and anxiety continue to hinder its control. This study assessed HPV-related knowledge, infection anxiety, and vaccine hesitancy among the adult population in Saudi Arabia (including both citizens and non-Saudi residents), as well as exposure to skin and genital warts. A total of 2014 adults (≥ 18 years) completed an online survey covering demographics, HPV knowledge, and the modified Generalized Anxiety Disorder-7 (GAD-7) scale adapted to measure HPV-specific concerns. Skin warts were common, particularly among females aged 21-50 years, whereas genital warts were less frequent, peaking in the 21-40 age group. Anxiety was highest among participants aged 18-30 years. Notable knowledge gaps existed regarding HPV transmission and vaccine benefits. Younger participants showed better understanding, but males were less aware of vaccine availability. Healthcare professionals and medical students demonstrated superior knowledge. Multinomial logistic regression showed that gender was the only significant predictor of vaccine willingness (p < 0.001). Males were more likely than females to say "yes" (aOR = 1.409, p = 0.009) and "no" (aOR = 1.633, p < 0.001), indicating that females were disproportionately hesitant ("not sure"). A high school education (vs. a medical background) was associated with lower willingness (aOR = 0.631, p = 0.030). Overall, the findings emphasize the need for targeted educational campaigns to improve HPV awareness and promote vaccine acceptance.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human papillomaviruses (HPV), their associated diseases (including cervical intraepithelial neoplasia and HPV-associated cancers), and vaccine hesitancy among adults in Saudi Arabia, focusing on knowledge gaps, infection anxiety, and determinants of vaccine acceptance.
Evidence base and method
The review synthesizes cross-sectional survey data from 2014 adults (≥18 years) in Saudi Arabia, including demographics, HPV knowledge assessments, and HPV-specific anxiety measured via a modified Generalized Anxiety Disorder-7 (GAD-7) scale. It employs multinomial logistic regression to identify predictors of vaccine willingness.
Consensus and conclusions
Younger participants demonstrated better HPV knowledge, while males exhibited lower awareness of vaccine availability and higher hesitancy. Healthcare professionals and medical students had superior knowledge. Gender was the only significant predictor of vaccine willingness (p < 0.001), with males more likely to accept or refuse vaccination compared to females.
Controversies and gaps
Knowledge gaps persisted regarding HPV transmission and vaccine benefits, particularly among non-medical backgrounds. The study did not address regional disparities, cultural barriers, or long-term behavioral outcomes post-intervention. Anxiety levels were highest in the 18-30 age group, but causal links to vaccine hesitancy were not established.
Research and practice implications
Targeted educational campaigns are needed to improve HPV awareness, particularly among males and non-medical populations. Surveillance should monitor vaccine acceptance trends and address misconceptions about HPV transmission and vaccine efficacy. Further research is required to evaluate the impact of anxiety on vaccine uptake and to design culturally tailored interventions.

阴茎上皮内瘤变和鳞状细胞癌:临床病理特征、分类和预后的当代回顾。

Penile Intraepithelial Neoplasia and Squamous Cell Carcinoma: A Contemporary Review of Clinicopathologic Features, Classification, and Prognostication.
作者： Matthew H Lanehart, Eric E Morgan, Colleen J. Beatty, Kody Heubach, Meaghan C. Dougher, Thomas Karrs
摘要中文翻译摘要 阴茎上皮内瘤变是一种罕见的癌前生殖器病变,如果没有适当的治疗管理,可能会进展为侵袭性阴茎鳞状细胞癌。最近由于生殖器 发病率 (HPV) 感染负担增加而导致 发病率 增加的趋势强调需要有效的诊断和管理策略。本文提供了临床病理学概述,重点关注先前未总结的肿瘤分类和预后的更新。我们根据 2022 年世界卫生组织指南检查分类和命名法的变化,并探讨 HPV 相关肿瘤和 HPV 独立肿瘤之间 发病机制 和预后的差异。本文强调需要更强有力的分子遗传学研究来进一步阐明预后生物标志物,并探讨免疫组织化学在分层亚型中日益明显的作用。此外,我们还通过一个说明性案例来强调准确的诊断和及时管理的重要性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述检查了 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 及其与阴茎上皮内瘤变 (PeIN) 和阴茎鳞状细胞癌 (PSCC) 的关联,重点关注 HPV 驱动的 发病机制 和分类更新如何为这些 HPV 相关生殖器病变的诊断和预后策略提供信息的核心问题。
证据基础与方法
该综述是当代临床病理学概述,根据 2022 年世界卫生组织指南总结了肿瘤分类和预后的更新,重点是区分 HPV 相关性和 HPV 独立性阴茎肿瘤。
共识与结论
该综述强调,阴茎上皮内瘤变是一种癌前病变,如果没有适当的治疗,有可能进展为浸润性阴茎鳞状细胞癌,并强调由于生殖器 HPV 感染负担增加,HPV 相关阴茎病变的 发病率 不断增加。
争议与缺口
该综述指出,需要进行更强有力的分子遗传学研究,以阐明预后生物标志物,并阐明免疫组织化学在 HPV 相关性与 HPV 独立性阴茎肿瘤亚型分层中的不同作用。
科研与实践启示
该综述强调了准确的 诊断 和及时治疗阴茎上皮内瘤变以防止进展为浸润性癌的重要性,并呼吁在 HPV 相关疾病 发病率 不断上升的背景下改进诊断和治疗策略。

Penile Intraepithelial Neoplasia and Squamous Cell Carcinoma: A Contemporary Review of Clinicopathologic Features, Classification, and Prognostication.

Authors: Matthew H Lanehart, Eric E Morgan, Colleen J. Beatty, Kody Heubach, Meaghan C. Dougher, Thomas Karrs
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Penile intraepithelial neoplasia is an uncommon precancerous genital lesion that may progress to invasive penile squamous cell carcinoma in the absence of proper therapeutic management. The recent trend of increasing incidence because of a heightened burden of genital human papillomavirus (HPV) infection emphasizes a need for effective diagnostic and management strategies. This article provides a clinicopathologic overview focusing on updates in tumor classification and prognostication not previously summarized. We examine changes in classification and nomenclature based on the 2022 World Health Organization guidelines and explore differences in pathogenesis and prognosis between HPV-associated and HPV-independent tumors. This article emphasizes the need for more robust molecular genetic studies to further elucidate prognostic biomarkers and explores the increasingly distinct role of immunohistochemistry in stratifying subtypes. Also, we present an illustrative case to highlight the significance of accurate diagnosis and prompt management.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human papillomaviruses (HPV) and their association with penile intraepithelial neoplasia (PeIN) and penile squamous cell carcinoma (PSCC), focusing on the central question of how HPV-driven pathogenesis and classification updates inform diagnostic and prognostic strategies for these HPV-associated genital lesions.
Evidence base and method
The review is a contemporary clinicopathologic overview summarizing updates in tumor classification and prognostication based on the 2022 World Health Organization guidelines, with a focus on distinguishing HPV-associated from HPV-independent penile tumors.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights that penile intraepithelial neoplasia is a precancerous lesion with potential progression to invasive penile squamous cell carcinoma without proper management, and emphasizes the increasing incidence of HPV-associated penile lesions due to rising genital HPV infection burden.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies a need for more robust molecular genetic studies to elucidate prognostic biomarkers and to clarify the distinct roles of immunohistochemistry in stratifying HPV-associated versus HPV-independent penile tumor subtypes.
Research and practice implications
The review underscores the importance of accurate diagnosis and prompt management of penile intraepithelial neoplasia to prevent progression to invasive carcinoma, and calls for improved diagnostic and management strategies in the context of rising HPV-associated disease incidence.

母胎医学学会特别声明:降低超声检查期间感染传播风险——2026年更新

Society for Maternal‐Fetal Medicine Special Statement: Reducing the risk of transmitting infection during ultrasound examination—Updated 2026
作者： Samuel T. Bauer, C. Andrew Combs, Society for Maternal‐Fetal Medicine (SMFM), SMFM Patient Safety and Quality Committee
摘要中文翻译摘要 超声检查可能在个体间传播感染。通过遵循某些已充分描述的程序,在检查之间清洁和消毒超声探头,可降低此类风险。然而,调查显示超声探头及其他设备的清洁和消毒存在普遍疏漏。2020年,母胎医学学会(SMFM)发布了一份患者安全指南,重点关注在经阴道超声检查中降低感染传播风险,强调选择对人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)具有已证实高效消毒效果的高水平消毒剂、选择具有文献记录的低泄漏率的探头套,以及对多次使用的耦合凝胶容器的警示。在本文中,SMFM发布了更新指南,其范围扩展至经腹部超声检查及超声引导的经皮和术中操作。其他更新包括一份较新的高水平消毒系统清单,以及引用近期文献中邻苯二甲醛对HPV的病毒灭活效果。该指南基于Spaulding感染风险分类,并与美国超声医学会、美国疾病控制与预防中心及美国食品药品监督管理局的建议保持一致。本特别声明取代母胎医学学会特别声明:2020年经阴道超声检查传播感染风险降低。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述关注人乳头瘤病毒(HPV),具体包括HPV16、HPV18、HPV31、HPV33、HPV45、HPV52及HPV58等高危型,及其与宫颈上皮内瘤变、HPV相关宫颈癌、HPV相关肛门癌、HPV相关口咽癌、生殖器疣及肛生殖器疣等疾病的关联。核心问题是如何在超声检查期间降低HPV传播风险,特别是经阴道、经腹部及超声引导操作。
证据基础与方法
本综述为母胎医学学会(SMFM)发布的更新指南,纳入了近期文献及新型消毒系统。其参考了Spaulding感染风险分类,并与美国超声医学会、美国疾病控制与预防中心及美国食品药品监督管理局的建议保持一致。综述包含调查数据,显示清洁和消毒实践存在疏漏。
共识与结论
在超声检查过程中传播 HPV 的风险可以通过在使用之间执行超声换能器清洁和消毒程序来降低。它强调使用已证明对 HPV 有效的高水平消毒剂,特别是邻苯二甲醛,以及低泄漏率换能器盖的重要性。该指南还警告不要使用多次使用的耦合凝胶容器。
争议与缺口
审查指出,调查显示换能器和其他设备的清洁和消毒方面普遍存在失误,这表明在遵守建议的做法方面存在差距。没有明确提及争议,但缺乏一致的实施表明实践中存在尚未解决的问题。
科研与实践启示
本综述建议更新消毒方案、采用新型高水平消毒系统,并确保医护人员接受适当培训,以预防HPV在超声操作期间的传播。同时强调需持续监测并严格遵循指南,以降低感染风险。

Society for Maternal‐Fetal Medicine Special Statement: Reducing the risk of transmitting infection during ultrasound examination—Updated 2026

Authors: Samuel T. Bauer, C. Andrew Combs, Society for Maternal‐Fetal Medicine (SMFM), SMFM Patient Safety and Quality Committee
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Ultrasound examinations have the potential to transmit infections between individuals. The risk can be reduced by following certain well‐described procedures to clean and disinfect ultrasound transducers between examinations. However, surveys have shown widespread lapses in the cleaning and disinfection of transducers and other equipment. In 2020, the Society for Maternal‐Fetal Medicine (SMFM) published a Patient Safety Guideline focused on reducing the risk of transmitting infection in transvaginal examinations, with emphasis on selection of high‐level disinfectants with proven efficacy against human papillomavirus (HPV), choice of transducer covers with documented low leakage rates, and cautions regarding multiuse containers of coupling gel. In this article, SMFM presents updated guidelines with an expanded scope that includes transabdominal examinations and ultrasound‐guided percutaneous and intraoperative procedures. Other updates include a list of newer high‐level disinfection systems and citation of recent literature on virucidal efficacy of ortho‐phthalaldehyde against HPV. The guidelines are based on the Spaulding classification of infection risk and are aligned with recommendations from the American Institute of Ultrasound in Medicine, the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention, and the US Food and Drug Administration. This Special Statement replaces Society for Maternal‐Fetal Medicine Special Statement: Reducing the risk of transmitting infection by transvaginal ultrasound examination, 2020 .
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on human papillomaviruses (HPV), specifically high-risk HPV types such as HPV16, HPV18, HPV31, HPV33, HPV45, HPV52, and HPV58, and their association with diseases like cervical intraepithelial neoplasia, HPV-associated cervical cancer, HPV-associated anal cancer, HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer, genital warts, and anogenital warts. The central question is how to reduce the risk of transmitting HPV during ultrasound examinations, particularly transvaginal, transabdominal, and ultrasound-guided procedures.
Evidence base and method
The review is an updated guideline from the Society for Maternal-Fetal Medicine (SMFM) that incorporates recent literature and new disinfection systems. It references the Spaulding classification of infection risk and aligns with recommendations from the American Institute of Ultrasound in Medicine, the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention, and the US Food and Drug Administration. The review includes surveys highlighting lapses in cleaning and disinfection practices.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that the risk of transmitting HPV during ultrasound examinations can be reduced by following procedures to clean and disinfect ultrasound transducers between uses. It emphasizes the use of high-level disinfectants with proven efficacy against HPV, particularly ortho-phthalaldehyde, and the importance of transducer covers with low leakage rates. The guidelines also caution against the use of multiuse containers of coupling gel.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that surveys have shown widespread lapses in the cleaning and disinfection of transducers and other equipment, indicating a gap in adherence to recommended practices. There is no explicit mention of controversies, but the lack of consistent implementation suggests unresolved issues in practice.
Research and practice implications
The review recommends updating disinfection protocols, using newer high-level disinfection systems, and ensuring proper training for healthcare providers to prevent HPV transmission during ultrasound procedures. It also highlights the need for ongoing surveillance and adherence to guidelines to reduce infection risks.
